<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt;color:#1F497D'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Rounding</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt'>Certain figures included in this Annual Report have been rounded to facilitate their presentation. Percentage figures have not always been calculated based on such rounded figures but on amounts prior to rounding. For this reason, percentage amounts may vary from those obtained by performing the same calculations using the figures of these consolidated Financial Statements. Certain numerical figures shown as totals in some tables may not be the arithmetic aggregation of the figures that precede them, due to rounding.</P>

</DIV>
<DIV style=margin-left:78pt;width:460.65pt><P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-78pt;margin-right:-21.25pt'><FONT style='font:11pt Verdana;color:#00B0F0'><I> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</I></FONT></P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:14pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:-56.7pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Main Financial Indicators</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:14pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:-56.7pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:7.1pt;margin-left:-78pt'><IMG src=tgs_9.jpg width=747 height=334 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:7.1pt;margin-left:-49.65pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:7.1pt;margin-left:-49.65pt'><IMG src=tgs_10.jpg width=683 height=425 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>8</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=font-size:11pt><B>1. </B><B>Letter to our Shareholders, Customers, Employees and Suppliers </B> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>It is with great enthusiasm that I share with you the accomplishments and progress we have reached throughout this year. It has been a period of major landmarks and sustained growth, achieved thanks to the effort and dedication of all the people who are part of our company.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>As we made our way through the 2024, we strengthened our commitment to innovation and energy development in Argentina. This drove us closer to the great dream we pursue: <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>To be leaders in the rendering of integrated services, acknowledged for our values, the quality of our </B><B>products</B><B> and services, our innovating spirit and the major role we play in the energy market of </B><B>Argentina </B><B>and the region</B><B>.</B></FONT> We keep evolving towards a culture that is open, dynamic, collaborative and digital. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Our results reflect our long-term business approach marked by both a cautious allocation of capital and investment in the development of Vaca Muerta and the safe and efficient operation of our businesses as well. We are present at the heart of Vaca Muerta, providing innovating and quality solutions for our clients, playing a major role in the consolidation of energy development in Argentina, to position our country as one of the main energy suppliers of the region. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Year after year, <B>tgs</B> shows enormous strength and resilience to address the never-ending macroeconomic challenges, including inflationary pressure, the regulatory framework and the high volatility of financial markets.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In the year 2024, we recorded revenues of Ps. 1,219,766 million, an operating income of 560,299 million and a net income of Ps. 370,163 million (Ps. 491.74 per share). These figures are compared to the results recorded in 2023, with net revenues of Ps. 986,053 million, an operating income of Ps. 253,558 million and a net income of Ps. 51,214 million (Ps. 68.03 per share).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>As further milestones of this year, it is important to highlight that Editorial Perfil awarded us with the <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Gold Fortune </B><B>P</B><B>rize to the best Argentine company</B></FONT> and that we celebrated our <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>30 </B><B>consecutive years listed in the New York </B><B>S</B><B>tock </B><B>M</B><B>arket</B></FONT>, one of the most important worldwide.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In the natural gas transportation segment, in 2024 we managed to obtain from the national government a 675% tariff increase as from the month of April, and subsequent monthly increases that have positioned tariffs at reasonable levels. This change in the tariff chart was reflected in the segment&#8217;s operating income of Ps. 220,453 million, versus the operating loss of Ps. 26,640 million recorded in 2023. Currently, we keep working with the corresponding entities to finalize the Five-Year Tariff Review (&#8220;RQT&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish) which will be concluded with the definition of a certainty framework for this business segment that ensures a profitable business and allows us to keep rendering a quality, safe and efficient service along with further works and investments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Furthermore, in 2024 we received a satisfactory report issued by ENARGAS, owing to which <B>tgs</B> expects to be awarded the extension of the License through which it conducts its operations until December 2047. This accomplishment further validates our operative and financial performance throughout our 33 years.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>9</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We re-assert our commitment and <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>keep investing in maintaining a safe and reliable natural gas transportation system</B></FONT>, consolidating <B>tgs</B> as the leading company in the natural gas transportation segment. Conducting the transportation of <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>60%</B><B> </B><B>of the natural gas consumed in </B><B>Argentina</B></FONT>, our main pipeline system satisfactorily met natural gas transportation demands. Thanks to our safe and reliable operations, our transportation system equipment reached operative efficiency and reliability ratios of 99.99%. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>To further demonstrate our commitment, it is worth noting the private initiative proposal we submitted to the Ministry of Economy for the investment of roughly US$ 500 million related to the expansion of the transportation capacity of the Perito Francisco Pascasio Moreno Pipeline (hereinafter: &#8220;GPM&#8221;; formerly: N&#233;stor Kirchner Pipeline or GPNK). Additionally, regardless of which company is awarded the private initiative, we pledged to invest US$ 200 million in the final sections of the pipeline we operate in the Natural Gas Transportation segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We kept strengthening our performance in the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment, consolidating ourselves as the second natural gas processor in Argentina. We have made the most of the quality of the natural gas arriving to the plant, which allowed us to keep our production levels above the million tons. It was indeed a great accomplishment for our team, who at the same time coordinated successfully the Cerri Complex major maintenance scheduled for this year, ensuring the continuity of our services while also complying with the technological revamping of our facilities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In spite of the major maintenance mentioned in the preceding paragraph, production levels reached 1,051,654 tons (82,643 lower than in 2023), consolidating our leadership in this business, with operative efficiency and reliability ratios of 98.4% and 98.1%, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>This business segment recorded an operating profit of 223,155 million, hence Ps. 36,393 million above 2023 figures. This positive variation was mainly attributable to the lower cost of the natural gas purchased as PTR, which was partially offset by a drop in revenues derived from a lesser impact of the  exchange rate and lower commercialized volumes. Regarding the average prices of propane, butane and natural gasoline they were up by 8%, 10% and 3%, respectively. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Our most challenging outlook is undoubtedly presented in the Midstream segment, where with steady steps and teamwork we managed to start up the first conditioning module of the Tratay&#233;n plant expansion that we had started in 2023. This work involves an approximate investment of US$ 350 million and with the Propak Systems Ltd (&#8220;Propak&#8221;) second conditioning module that went into operation by the end of February, 2025, will raise our aggregate conditioning capacity to 28 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/day, enabling us to provide conditioning services in the Vaca Muerta System, ensuring producers&#8217; gas volumes evacuation. This project also posits further opportunities as it allows the extraction of liquids with a low additional investment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>The opportunities that arise from the development of Vaca Muerta have presented great challenges to us and fuel our dream of being the leading company in the rendering of integrated services, acknowledged by our values, products and services quality, innovative spirit and leadership in the energy market in Argentina and the region. We thus reassert our commitment to the planet and the community where we conduct our businesses.</FONT> </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>10</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'>None of our accomplishments would have been possible without the dedication and talent of the 1,147 members of our team. Our people, in the face of complex and challenging times, have risen to adapt and overcome each of the hurdles. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We keep evolving towards an open, dynamic and cooperative culture, working in line with the highest health and safety standards, giving priority to the wellbeing of all the people with whom we relate. We promote diversity and equity and create long-lasting bonds that encourage the professional growth of our workers.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We understand that the new challenges require innovation, flexibility and a cooperative environment. We thus started our path towards the digital transformation that does not only imply a technological updating but also a further step forward into the cultural transformation required by the opportunities that come our way. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We keep improving our performance in Environment, Social and Governance (ESG) matters. In 2024, we conducted a double materiality process. After gathering our stakeholders&#8217; groups feedback through a public survey, we developed our matrix of impacts, risks and opportunities, on which our ESG strategy was outlined. This strategy is based on the following cornerstones: (i) preserving the environment, minimizing impact, (ii) managing risks, boosting opportunities, (iii) developing our people and communities and (iv) promoting institutional soundness. This will be our compass in the upcoming years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Our future outlook</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We are present at the root of Vaca Muerta with an infrastructure that is unique in the region. We are convinced that to ensure Argentina&#8217;s leading role in the future of energy, we must provide solutions to gas and oil producers, adding value and taking their products to consumers.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=color:#000000>The future of natural gas as an essential fuel in the country&#8217;s energy matrix and as an energy transition fuel gets stronger and stronger every year, which will turn Argentina into a sustainable country of great growth. <B>tgs</B> has consolidated as an </FONT><B>integrated services provider in the hydrocarbon industry.</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>On that ground, we are assessing a project that allows us to adapt our Tratay&#233;n plant into a facility similar to the Cerri Plant, with a processing capacity of around 40MMm3/d. This project is to be complemented with the construction of a polyduct from Tratay&#233;n to Bah&#237;a Blanca for the transportation of liquids to the fractionating and dispatch facilities to be built for the aggregate production volume exports.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>This further underscores our commitment to the energy development and growth of our country, via key infrastructure that boosts the growth of Vaca Muerta, confident that given an appropriate economic and regulatory framework, we can turn Argentina into one of the main energy suppliers of the region and position <B>tgs</B> as a leading and innovating midstream services company. Our yearly achievements, along with the future projects that lie ahead of us, are proof of the operative and financial soundness our company has achieved over its track record.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>11</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Never forgetting the role we play as renderers of the public natural gas transportation service; we are fully aware of the utmost importance of continuing with the required investments under an adequate regulatory framework.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2025, we will keep strengthening our commitment to the sustainable management of our business, seeking to introduce ESG material issues into our strategy and decision- making, focusing on the identified sustainability risks and opportunities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>As we look ahead to a challenging 2025, we firmly believe we will fulfill all the goals we have set out, working with passion and focusing on our clients and the community. Our deepest commitment will be to contribute to the energy development of Argentina.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We thank our shareholders for their trust in our performance, our clients and suppliers for being part of the management of our businesses and our workforce for their tireless commitment and dedication. To them we owe our positioning at the highest performance standards throughout our history of 33 years, which &#8212;empowered by our cultural change process&#8212; will allow <B>tgs</B> to be at the forefront of our industry.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:283.2pt;margin-right:-2.25pt'> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; Luis Fallo</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:283.2pt;margin-right:-2.25pt;color:#000000'>Vice-chairman acting as Chairman </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>12</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>2. Macroeconomic Scenario</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>2.1 Argentine Context</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We are conducting our operations amidst a complex economic context, the main variables of which have recently suffered high volatility, as a consequence of political and economic developments within the national scope.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>On December 10, 2023, a new administration led by Javier Milei in the Executive Branch (&#8220;PEN&#8221;, according to its initials in Spanish) took office, signaling a sharp turn from the road pursued by the country in the previous years in terms of economy, politics, and international policies. Given the unfavorable macroeconomic conditions as well as the uncertain governability of the new administration, upon taking office the new government adopted a set of measures under the framework of an emergency scheme. The emergency measures targeted the elimination of the fiscal deficit (via a strong fiscal adjustment), zero monetary issuance by the Central Bank of the Argentine Republic (&#8220;BCRA&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish) to achieve an ensuing drop in inflation and the readjustment of relative prices, particularly of the public utilities&#8217; fees. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Along with these measures, the government issued a set of reforms, among which we can point out at the Argentine state deregulation decree, the fiscal law, and probably the most relevant of all: the submittal of the Law of Bases to the Congress. After six months of debate in Congress, on June 28 2024 the Chamber of Deputies gave green light to the definite text of the Law of Bases, which had formerly obtained half approval from the Senate. Among other issues, the mentioned bill includes:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The declaration of a public emergency in administrative, economic, financial and energy matters for the term of one year.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Amendments in the calculation of income taxes for individuals, the self-employment tax regime, personal assets and money laundering.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The approval of the privatization, whether total or partial, of certain companies and partnerships fully or partially owned by the National Government.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The creation of an Incentive Regime for Large Investments (&#8220;RIGI&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish) for projects that meet certain conditions. RIGI grants some fiscal, custom and exchange benefits with the aim of encouraging private investment.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Amendments to the labor and retirement regime &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Amendments to Natural Gas Law N&#176; 24,076 to, among other issues, allow the extension of the license for an additional term of 20 years (versus the ten years of extension originally stipulated). &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Amendments to the Hydrocarbons Law N&#176; 17,319 to provide legal security to the energy development of Argentina and ensure free commerce and competition.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Law of Bases has been disputed by several sectors of the economy and the political spectrum and has even been challenged at court. In spite of that, the current administration considers it of the utmost importance for the country&#8217;s restructuring and economic and institutional recovery. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Although some of the reforms proposed by the Law of Bases have already been regulated and implemented, others are going through their regulation and legislative discussion processes. Therefore, </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>13</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>as of the date of the issuance of the present Annual Report it is not possible to foresee the impact that the Law of Bases and the fiscal package might have on the results, financial condition and cash flows of the Company. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Among the main 2024 macroeconomic variables, it is worth pointing out at:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The CPI recorded in 2024 was of 117.8%, whereas it had been of 211.4% in 2023. As soon as the new administration&#8217;s took office on December 10, 2023 it raised the official exchange rate from $366 to $800 and December 2023 monthly inflation recorded a 25.5% variation, the highest in the last 3 decades. In 2024, after recording increases of 20.6%, 13.2% and 11% for the first 3 months of the year, inflation rates started to slow down to hit 2.7% in October, the lowest rate in 3 years.<FONT style=font-family:Helvetica;color:#444444;background-color:#FFFFFF>&#160;</FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Internal Index&#160;Wholesale Prices (IPIM, according to its acronym in Spanish) for the year 2024 was at 67.1%, compared to the accumulation of 276.4% recorded in the year 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>After the abrupt devaluation of the Argentine peso on December 12, 2023 mentioned above, the Argentine government defined a path of a stable 2% monthly devaluation with regard to the United States currency. According to BNA issuances, the exchange rate closed the year 2024 at $ 1,032.00 per each United States dollar, whereas as of December 31, 2023 the exchange rate had been at $ 808.45 (representing an increase of around 27.7%, below inflation measured by the CPI). Regarding the exchange rate gap, upon the new administration&#8217;s coming to office, it was above 150% whereas at the closing of the year 2024 it had decreased to roughly 13%.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The country risk premium paid by Argentine debt, measured by the Emerging Markets Bonds Index (EMBI+) prepared by JP Morgan, had been around 1,906 points as of December 31, 2023 whereas by December 31, 2024 it had dropped to 635 points.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:8pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Economic activity: GDP fell by 2.1% in the third quarter of 2024. On the other hand, in November 2024 the Monthly Estimate of Economic Activity (&#8220;EMAE&#8221;, according to its initials in Spanish) recorded a 0.9% year-on-year drop. Throughout the 2024, this indicator&#8217;s behavior was uneven and heterogeneous, depending on each economic sector. Whereas energy (favored by the development of Vaca Muerta) and mining showed the most significant increases, construction, industry and commerce fell on a year-on-year comparison.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:8pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The Salary Index as of December 2024 recorded a year-on-year increase of 145.5%. It is worth underlining the unevenness of the salary evolution of private workers compared to state employees and unregistered workers. Something similar happened to the unemployment rate, which rose to 6.9% in the third quarter of 2024.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.7pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>BCRA reserves remained under pressure despite the growing restrictions in the access to the exchange market. As of December 31, 2024, net reserves reached US$ 29,612 million, from US$ 23,073 million recorded in December 2023. &nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>14</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The trade balance of 2024 recorded a US$ 18,899 surplus, whereas it had showed a deficit of US$ 6,925 million in 2023. This was mainly driven by an increase in exports in the agricultural and energy sectors and a drop in imports.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Since their first month of administration, the government of Javier Milei has managed to reverse the fiscal deficit, reaching a primary and financial surplus for the first time in 14 years. The financial surplus of $1.8 billion was equivalent to 0.3% of the GDP. On the other hand, the primary surplus rose to 1.8% of the GDP. This was attributable to a 27% decrease in primary expenses.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The outlook for next year is challenging, as the political arena will witness an electoral year with October legislative elections to renew part of the Chamber of Deputies and Senators in the National Congress. The government&#8217;s challenge will lie in its capacity to maintain the fiscal surplus and thus keep attracting investors&#8217; trust, consolidate the improvement in economic variables and at the same time achieve a recovery in the economic activity to consolidate growth.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Management of the Company is permanently monitoring the evolution of the variables that may have impact on our business, in order to outline business plans and identify the possible impacts on <B>tgs</B> financial condition. The present Annual Report must be read in light of the circumstances mentioned above.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>2.2 International Scenario</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The world economic context in 2024 presented a global economic slowdown due to political uncertainties and geopolitical tensions. The reelection of Donald Trump in the United States and the war in the Middle East were key factors in that sense. Protectionist policies and commercial conflicts had an impact on global commerce, deriving in volatility in financial markets. According to the World Economy Outlook Report issued by the IMF, world GDP es expected to have gone up by 3.2% in 2024, being foreseen to grow at a similar pace during 2025. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The cooling down of inflation remained one of the main concerns that governments worldwide had to address throughout the year: in 2024, worldwide inflation showed a downward trend, going down from 6.8% in 2023 to 5.9%. Main economies addressed the issue with restrictive monetary policies, raising interest rates to control inflation. United States and the European union could significantly reduce their inflation rates, getting close to their targets, whereas emerging and developing economies faced greater challenges due to inflationary pressure persistence. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Commodities prices have suffered significant fluctuations. Gold hit new highest values due to its status as safe haven in times of uncertainty. Metals that are essential for energy transition, such as copper and lithium, also rose due to an increasing demand for them.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Oil price remained volatile. Tensions in the Middle East and energy policies in the United States heavily influenced global supply and demand. Despite efforts to decarbonize the economy, the demand for oil and its byproducts remained high, driving prices up at several moments of the year.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>15</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Regarding average international prices of propane, butane, and natural gasoline, they went up by 10%, 11% and 2% in 2024, respectively, compared to the previous year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Despite their decreasing trend, energy commodities prices are still high compared to levels prior to the pandemic. This is due to the fact that supply is still limited, as new investments in energy infrastructures have been deficient over the last years. The energy commodities price outlook for 2024 is blurry: the evolution of the war in Ukraine, worldwide economic recovery and weather conditions are the main factors that will define price behavior.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Global financial markets showed high volatility. Central banks interest rate decreases sought to boost economic growth, but also generated uncertainty among investors.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2024, the spotlight was on inflation, on central banks&#8217; responses and global elections. As we head towards the 2025, although economic cycle dynamics remain crucial for the global outlook, focus will shift to United States policies in areas such as commerce, immigration, fiscal and regulatory policies. The road to disinflation will continue, though at a lower speed: limiting central banks&#8217; actions. Also, a potential slowdown in China&#8217;s growth, the evolution of its relation with the United States, as well as the development of armed conflicts might derive in a high level of uncertainty and an increase in volatility.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:1pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B>3. Natural Gas Industry in Argentina</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;text-indent:-14.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Demand for natural gas</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Natural gas maintains its predominant share in the national energy matrix<FONT style=font-size:8pt><A name=_ftnb2 /><A href=#_ftnf2><SUP>2</SUP></A></FONT>, accounting for 52% out of total fuels consumed, in accordance with data provided by the National 2023 Energy Balance.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><IMG src=tgs_11.jpg width=425 height=230 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: Secretariat of Energy&#8211; Energy Balance.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Natural gas consumption throughout the year 2024 reached 137 MMm3/d, recording a 1.9% increase in natural gas consumption versus the previous year (+2.5 MMm3/d). This variation was mainly attributable to an increase in thermal power plants consumption &#8211;given greater availability of natural </P>
<HR style=text-align:left;width:25%><P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0'><A name=_ftnf2 /><A href=#_ftnb2><SUP>2</SUP></A> The energy matrix corresponds to the domestic energy supply (production plus imports less exports + losses).</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>16</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>gas for power stations- and, to a lesser extent, product of a slight increase of priority demand derived from lower temperatures during winter months (May, July, and August) and higher exports volumes. </FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_12.jpg width=519 height=270 alt='Imagen 55' title='Imagen 55'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: Our own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The winter of 2024 average temperatures<FONT style=font-size:8pt><A name=_ftnb3 /><A href=#_ftnf3><SUP>3</SUP></A></FONT> were colder (13,3&#176;C) than the ones corresponding to the same period in 2023 (14.2&#176;C), which explains the slight increase in the priority demand (residential + SMEs) compared to the previous year. The total increase recorded in 2024 versus the previous year was of 0.7 MMm3/d, which was complemented by a 0.6% annual increase of the number of residential users (November 2024 vs November 2023), totaling 8.88 million users. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Production levels have shown an upward trend over the last years, which has continued throughout the 2024, thanks to the Gas.Ar Plan incentives scheme and the transportation capacity increase with the Tratay&#233;n &#8211;&nbsp;Salliquel&#243; pipeline startup in 2023. Such higher natural gas availability in the domestic market allows exports, mainly to Chile, through the Gas Andes pipeline even in winter months, taking advantage of the fact that given the current transportation infrastructure such delivery would not restrict supply to the domestic market. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The demand from the electricity sector showed a 0.5% drop compared to the previous year, whereas total electricity generation increased in the same percentage (+0,7 TWh). The increase in domestic generation is attributable mainly to higher renewable energies (+2,8 TWh), contributing to the system a 16.1% of total generation (142,1 TWh). Likewise, higher nuclear generation was recorded (+1,5 TWh) mainly owing to the higher contribution from the Atucha II nuclear plant and a higher thermal generation (+2,4 TWh). The increase in the generation of both technologies was partially offset by a lower hydraulic contribution (-5,9 TWh). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The graph below presents the monthly consumption of the year 2024 in MMm3/d for each of the demand sectors, compared to the total demand corresponding to the year 2023 (represented by the red line). </P>
<HR style=text-align:left;width:25%><P style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0'><A name=_ftnf3 /><A href=#_ftnb3><SUP>3</SUP></A> Buenos Aires Ring&#8217;s average temperature for the winter period (May to September). &nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>17</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_13.jpg width=585 height=259 alt='Imagen 58' title='Imagen 58'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: <B>tgs</B>&#8217; own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Demand for natural gas in 2024 was above the levels recorded the previous year. A breakdown analysis shows that variations in consumption are mainly attributable to lower temperatures recorded in winter months; mainly in the month of May, and to a lesser extent in the months of July and August. In turn, the higher availability of natural gas and more moderate temperatures allowed a greater consumption by electricity power stations in the months of September, October and November. The months with lowest consumptions in relation with the previous year were January and June. The sectors with lowest consumption during 2024 as a result of lower activity levels (drop in GDP) were industry and CNG. Regarding the industry sector, in September 2024 it could recover 2023 consumption levels and increase them as from that moment. In connection with CNG, average values stably remained 0.4 MMm3/d below the previous year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The graph below presents 2024 monthly variations as compared to 2023, in MMm3/d for each of the demand sectors.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>18</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural gas consumption variation per sector (</B><B>2024</B><B> vs. </B><B>2023</B><B>)</B></FONT> </P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_14.jpg width=585 height=309 alt='Imagen 6' title='Imagen 6'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: <B>tgs</B>&#8217; own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2024, based on the total breakdown of natural gas consumption per kind of user in Argentina, it is observed that the sector with the highest demand for natural gas was electricity power plants, followed secondly by priority demand, whereas the industrial sector ranked third.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B>NATURAL GAS CONSUMPTION PER KIND OF USER (YEAR 2024)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><IMG src=tgs_15.jpg width=482 height=223 alt='Picture 5' title='Picture 5'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#808080'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: Our own preparation with in-company data.</FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>19</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Natural Gas Supply</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Gas.Ar Plan has been implemented since the year 2021, underlining the need to guarantee the supply of natural gas and outlining incentives to facilitate immediate investments to keep up and/or increase production in gas basins, where producers commit to achieve a production curve that guarantees current levels maintenance and/or growth. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The program articulates a mechanism that guarantees the satisfaction of gas demand from priority residential consumers and electricity power plants, allowing exports of the unused balances in non-winter seasons. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The first rounds of the tender for capacities and prices under this program were conducted during 2020 and 2021, showing producers&#8217; interest to increase their activities. Afterwards, through Res. N&#176; 770/2022, on November 11, 2022, the Secretariat of Energy summoned to a National Invitation to Tender for the extension of the commitments undertaken by the successful bidders in the Neuqu&#233;n basin in Rounds 1 and 3 (Round 4.1), as well as the awarding of the following incremental volumes in the Neuqu&#233;n basin (Round 4.2) with the extension of the term until December 31, 2028:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:7.1pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>1)&#160;Base Gas July: up to 11 MMm3/d for the period comprised from July 1, 2023 to December 31, 2028; 3.41 USD/MMBTU (weighted average price in accordance with results issued by the Secretariat of Energy Resolution No. 860/2022).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:7.1pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>2)&#160;Base Gas January: up to 3MMm3/d for the period comprised from January 1, 2024 and December 31, 2028; 2.99 USD/MMBTU (weighted average price in accordance with results issued by the Secretariat of Energy Resolution No. 860/2022).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:7.1pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>3)&#160;Peak Gas 2024: up to 7 MMm3/d for the period comprised from May 1 and September 30 of the years 2024 to 2028; 5.52 USD/MMBTU (weighted average price in accordance with results issued by the Secretariat of Energy Resolution No. 860/2022).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:7.1pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>4)&#160;Peak Gas 2025: up to 7 MMm3/d for the period comprised from May 1 and September 30 of the years 2025 to 2028. 4.68 USD/MMBTU (weighted average price in accordance with results issued by the Secretariat of Energy Resolution No. 860/2022).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style=color:#000000>In 2024, aggregate natural gas production went up above levels recorded the previous year, going up by 5,1% (138,6 MMm3/d vs 131,9 MMm3/d). </FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>20</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural gas production per basin (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: SESCO, SE.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In a breakdown of natural gas operators, in 2024 YPF continued being the main producer of Argentina, with its share of 24% of the total (-2% vs 2023), followed by Total Austral S.A. with 22%. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:70.8pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Main Argentine natural gas operators&#8217; share over total production (2024)</B></P>
<P align=right style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:70.8pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_16.jpg width=474 height=256 alt='Imagen 20' title='Imagen 20'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#808080'>Source: SESCO, SE</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Ninth Addendum to the Gas import Agreement (started in 2006) between Energ&#237;a Argentina (ENARSA) and YPFB (Bolivian Fiscal Oil Fields) was entered on June 16, 2024 and it extends the delivery of natural gas volumes for the months of August and September. By means of said Addendum, it was agreed, along with the finalization of the deliveries, the import of further 4 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d &#8212;given the importance for Argentina to supply its northwest provinces until the Northern Pipeline reversal works are finalized&#8212;. In November, the import of small gas volumes from Bolivia was carried out in an interruptible modality. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>21</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Supply from Bolivia recorded an average 3.2 MMm3/d volume, 3.0 MMm3/d lower than the supply recorded in 2023. On the other hand, LNG imports by sea, re-gasified and injected into the natural gas transportation system at the Escobar port located in the Province of Buenos Aires, recorded an average contribution of 4.3 MMm3/d in 2024, thus 2.5 MMm3/d below LNG contribution recorded in 2023 at both terminals (Escobar and Bah&#237;a Blanca). Additionally, Enarsa agreed with the Chilean state company Enap the import of natural gas volumes from the Mejillones LNG regasification terminal in Chile, which enters Argentina through the NorAndino Pipeline, with the aim of supplying our country northern area. </P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural Gas Imports (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: <B>tgs</B>&#8217; own elaboration based on in-company data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style=color:#000000>Total natural gas injection in August 2024, highest injection level of the year, reached 143 MMm3/d, therefore 10 MMm3/d above the highest level recorded in 2023.</FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Supply (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_17.jpg width=546 height=259 alt='Imagen 22' title='Imagen 22'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: <B>tgs</B>&#8217; own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>22</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Neuqu&#233;n Basin has the highest natural gas injection volume, whereas the remaining basins continued their natural decline (even Bolivia is undergoing a decline process), which makes it imperative to build the third main pipeline to transport the incremental gas proceeding from the Neuqu&#233;n basin reserves developments. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Local injection (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: <B>tgs</B>&#8217; own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On February 7, 2022 the Secretariat of Energy issued Res. N&#176; 67/2022, by means of which it creates the Pipeline System Program known as &#8220;Transport.Ar National Production&#8221; and declared as matters of national public interest: the construction of the President N&#233;stor Kirchner pipeline, the works required to expand the capacity of the National Natural Gas Transportation System -such as the pipeline linking the cities of Mercedes and Cardales in the Province of Buenos Aires-, and the expansion of the Neuba II Pipeline.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, by way of Decree N&#176; 76/2022 dated February 11, 2022, the government granted IEASA the license to operate this pipeline for a 35-year term. It also created the Argentine Gas Development Fund (&#8220;FONDESGAS&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish)-, appointing IEASA as both trustor and beneficiary and BICE as trustee (Argentinean Development&#160;Bank&#160;of&#160;Investment&#160;and&#160;Foreign Trade: &#8220;BICE&#8221; for its acronym in Spanish) </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The construction of the Perito Moreno Pipeline (former President N&#233;stor Kirchner Pipeline) &#8212;and its complementary works&#8212; is of vital importance for the energetic development of Argentina and will generate both fiscal benefits and a saving in foreign currency for Argentina as well.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In its first stage, it will link the city of Tratay&#233;n in the Province of Neuqu&#233;n with the city of Salliquel&#243; in the Province of Buenos Aires, where it will be connected to the pipeline system operated by <B>tgs, </B>contributing with a<B> </B>21 MM3/d transportation capacity, with the construction of a section of the pipeline and two compressor plants, the first in Tratay&#233;n and the second in Salliquel&#243;.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>23</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On June 19, 2024, <B>tgs</B> submitted a Private Initiative Proposal to the Ministry of Economy for the execution of the Project known as Natural Gas Transportation Capacity Increase, for the Tratay&#233;n &#8211;&nbsp;Salliquel&#243; route. Said Project was declared of National Public Interest by the Argentine National Government via Decree N&#176;<FONT style='color:#0000FF;border-bottom:1px solid #0000FF'> 1,060/2024</FONT> issued in the Official Gazette in its edition dated December 2, 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Project seeks to increase the natural gas transportation capacity in the Tratay&#233;n &#8211;&nbsp;Salliquel&#243; route by 14 MMm3/d to reach 35 MMm3/d of natural gas transportation capacity in the Section I of GPFPM (the current 21 MMm&#179;/d transportation capacity plus the 14 MMm&#179;/d incremental transportation capacity), maximizing flow from production basins to consumption centers.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style=color:#000000>This Project is expected to have a positive impact on the domestic market, improve energy safety and reduce dependance on liquid fuels and Liquefied Natural Gas (LNG), which are more expensive and less environment-friendly. Besides, the greater capacity will enable us to meet the growing demand in a more efficient manner, benefiting all kind of consumers. The Project can also foster competition in the supply of natural gas, reducing market concentration, which would be translated into lower prices for consumers. This positive impact would also be reflected in fiscal savings for around US$ 500 million as a result of lower imports of LNG and liquid fuels, lower pressure on energy subsidies and a substantial improvement in our country trade balance of about US$ 700 million.</FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:1pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4. OUR BUSINESSES</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We are present throughout the entire natural gas production process in Argentina, from the well-head to the transportation and distribution systems. &nbsp;We are an energy company that is part of the whole natural gas supply chain, providing services and solutions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Since 2018, we have betted on the development of the Argentine market. Our insight, added to our expertise of over thirty years, is what guarantees the excellence of our services.&#160;&#160;&#160;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>24</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-42.55pt'><B><IMG src=tgs_18.jpg width=674 height=318 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'></B>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:70.9pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_22.jpg width=440 height=295 alt='Imagen 532785800' title='Imagen 532785800'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
