<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>26</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Tariff Overview</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It should be noted that the operation of pipelines requires a high level of investments related to the service&#8217;s quality, safety and reliability. There lies the importance of agreeing on a natural gas transportation tariff based on an economic and efficient operation that generates enough revenues to render a sustainable, safe and reliable service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>For that reason, we continue with our proceedings before the ENARGAS authorities that took office on December 28, 2023 to obtain a transitory tariff adjustment and the finalization of the Five-Year Tariff Review Process. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On December 14, 2023, through Resolution N&#176; 704/2023 ENARGAS summoned a Public Hearing to be held on January 8, 2024 with the objective of addressing a transitory tariff adjustment. As a result of said hearing, on February 15, 2024 ENARGAS Resolution N&#176; 52/2024 was issued, by means of which the validity of the Public Hearing was ratified and ENARGAS stated that the transitory tariff charts that arose from the Hearing would be issued within 30 working days as from the date of said Resolution.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Alongside these events, on December 16, 2023, Decree N&#176; 55/2023 was issued, by means of which the Argentine national energy sector&#8217;s state of emergency was declared until December 31, 2024, which was extended until July 9, 2025, by Decree No. 1023/2024 of November 19, 2024. Among other issues, this Decree: (i) stipulated the commencement of the five-year tariff review process, (ii) dictated the intervention of ENARGAS as from January 1&#176;, 2024 and (iii) instructed the Secretariat of Energy to issue the regulations and procedures required to sanction market prices for the natural gas transportation service. Decree N&#186; 1023/2024 dictated the coming into force of the tariff charts resulting from the tariff review based on the stipulations of Decree N&#176; 55/23, which should not be later than July 9, 2025.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On March 26, 2024, <B>tgs</B> entered the 2024 Transitory Agreement with ENARGAS, which outlined a transitory adjustment of 675% in natural gas transportation tariffs. This tariff increase came into effect on April 3, 2024, after the issuance of ENARGAS Resolution N&#176; 112/2024 (&quot;<B>Resolution 112</B>&quot;) in the Official Gazette. In line with Resolution 112, as from May 2024 and until the Tariff Review Process has been completed, tariffs would be monthly adjusted by a composite index (the &#8220;<B>Transitory Composite Index</B>&#8221;) made up as follows:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:49.65pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;text-align:right;margin-left:-49.65pt;width:49.65pt;font:9pt Verdana'>i.</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:21.15pt></KBD>By the Salary Index in a 47%&#8211; Registered Private Sector issued by the INDEC.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:49.65pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;text-align:right;margin-left:-49.65pt;width:49.65pt;font:9pt Verdana'>ii.</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:21.15pt></KBD>By the IPIM in a 27.2%; and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:49.65pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;text-align:right;margin-left:-49.65pt;width:49.65pt;font:9pt Verdana'>iii.</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:21.15pt></KBD>By the INDEC Construction Cost Index of Greater Buenos Aires-Materials section in the remaining 25.8%&#8211; &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>For the purposes of such adjustment, ENARGAS indicated that it would issue the corresponding monthly resolution, adjusting the tariff charts to be applied. In addition, this new transitory agreement eliminates the restriction on the payment of dividends previously contained.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>During the months from May to July 2024, ENARGAS informed the Company that it would delay the implementation of the monthly tariff adjustment. It also notified us that it would replace the monthly adjustment methodology mentioned above for the remainder of the 2024. Based on said ENARGAS </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>27</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>notice, the monthly tariff increase would be based on expected inflation, to be estimated by the Ministry of Economy for said period. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Also, on July 1, ENARGAS again informed the Company of the postponement of the monthly tariff increase, this time corresponding to the month of July, maintaining the tariff charts in effect as from April 3, 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Therefore, throughout the 2024 and the period elapsed in 2025, the Company received tariff increases of 675%, 4%, 1%, 2,7%, 3,5%, 3%, 2,5% and 1,5% effective as from April 3, August 1, September 2, October 1, November 4, December 4 of 2024, January 1 and February 1 of 2025, respectively. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>It should be noted that the operation of gas pipelines by tgs requires a high level of investments related to the quality, safety and reliability of the service. This is why it is important to determine the tariff for the public natural gas transportation service on the basis of a prudent and efficient economic operation, which allows obtaining sufficient income to provide a sustainable, safe and reliable service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Within the framework of the Five-Year Tariff Review process, on January 14, 2025, ENARGAS, through Resolution N&#176; 16/2025, summoned a public hearing that was celebrated on February 6, 2025. The aim of said hearing was to submit for consideration, among other issues, the Natural Gas Transportation and Distribution Five-Year Tariff Review, as well as the periodic adjustment of the natural gas transportation and distribution tariffs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>At said hearing, <B>tgs</B> presented, among other issues, its 2025-2029 Five-Year Expenses and Investments Plan, its Capital Base and its &#8220;WACC&#8221; Proposal _Weighted Average Cost of Capital_ (9.98% after tax). Taking into account the tariff calculation methodology and the mentioned parameters, we requested a tariff increase of 22.7% with regard to the tariffs effective as of January, 2025. We also presented alternative tariff adjustment methodologies:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>IPIM, or&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>A polynomial formula based on information issued by the INDEC:&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:72pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Courier New;margin-left:-18pt'>&#111;</KBD>30% IPIM,&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:72pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Courier New;margin-left:-18pt'>&#111;</KBD>40% of total registered salary index, and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:72pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Courier New;margin-left:-18pt'>&#111;</KBD>30% of construction cost index, materials sector.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>As of the date of the issuance of the present Annual Report, ENARGAS has not issued the resolution that concludes the Five-Year Tariff Review, granting the requested tariff increase and outlining the framework under which the natural gas transportation activity will be carried out during the 2025-2029 five-year term. However, at the Public Hearing, ENARGAS proposed the application of an actual WACC rate of 7.18%, after taxes, and a periodic adjustment proposal made up by 50% CPI and 50% IPIM.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On the subject of the License, the original term of the natural gas transportation system operation license would expire in December 2027 but could be extended by the Executive Branch for an additional term of 10 years, as long as certain technical conditions outlined therein are met. On September 8, </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>28</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>2023, <B>tgs</B> presented a request to ENARGAS to start the procedure outlined under Section 6 of the Natural Gas Law N&#176; 24,076 for the renewal of the mentioned license.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Additionally, in accordance with the provisions of the Law of Bases, the mentioned extension of 10 years has been increased to 20 years. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On June 13, 2024, ENARGAS issued a technical and legal report indicating the Company has fully met its obligations regarding the License. Said report entitled an ENARGAS controller &#8212;after the non-binding Public Hearing held on October 21, 2024&#8212; to issue the recommendation report to be presented to the Executive Branch so that the latter may finally grant the license extension. Upon the presentation of ENARGAS recommendation, the Executive Branch has the deadline of 120 working days to issue the decree extending the license. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Private Initiative</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>In June 2024 we submitted to the national government a private initiative execution proposal (hereinafter &#8220;Private Initiative&#8221;) related to the GPM, along with a commitment to invest in the regulated system, both of which in aggregate add up to around US$ 700 million. This proposal was declared of national public interest in December 2024 by way of Decree N&#176; 1060/2024. In the Private Initiative, <B>tgs</B> proposes: </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>The expansion of the GPM transportation capacity by 14 MMm3/d, involving an investment of around U$S 500 million, for which in December 2024 the Dedicated Branch I was registered, so that said project could be framed under the Incentive Regime for Large Investments (&#8220;RIGI&#8221; according to its acronym in Spanish). The resulting transportation capacity expansion, as outlined by the Decree N&#176; 54/2025, would fall within the framework of the GPM Transportation License granted to ENARSA, which according to Executive Branch Decree N&#176; 76/2022, was conducted in line with the provisions of the Law of Hydrocarbons N&#176; 17,319, and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Notwithstanding the outcome of the Private Initiative tender, we undertake the commitment to conduct an investment on the regulated system that we currently operate in the natural gas transportation segment that complements said initiative. This expansion is to be carried out through the construction of 20 km of pipe loops and the installation of 15,000 HP compression facilities in the Neuba II pipeline, added to other works and tests to increase the maximum operation pressure. This estimated further investment, which will allow us to expand by 12 MMm3/d the transportation capacity in the final sections, will be around US$ 200 million.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>The main objective of this work is to generate significant foreign currency savings for Argentina, as it would substitute gasoil and LNG imports with Vaca Muerta gas in winter, thus guaranteeing supply during colder weather seasons.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On November 4, 2024, the Board of Directors of the Company decided to constitute the Transportadora de Gas del Sur S.A. Dedicated Branch 1 (hereinafter &#8220;Dedicated Branch&#8221;), in order to adhere to the Large Investment Incentive Regime (&#8220;RIGI&#8221;) to execute this project. In December 2024, our Dedicated Branch was registered with the Inspection Board of Legal Entities (&#8220;IGJ&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish).</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>29</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On February 25, 2025, the Ministry of Economy issued Resolution No. 169/2025 by which it delegates to the Energy Secretary and ENARSA the responsibility for carrying out the bidding of the Private Initiative. The Resolution also establishes a series of guidelines which ENARSA will have to consider when preparing the bidding documents. As of the date of issuance of this Annual Report, the Private Initiative bidding for the awarding of the new transportation capacity resulting from the expansion work has not been launched.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Operation of the Natural gas transportation system </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, the average daily natural gas injection to the pipeline system operated by tgs amounted to 83.4 MMm3/d, hence 10.1% higher than the value recorded in 2023. Such injection includes contributions from the Austral, San Jorge Gulf and Neuqu&#233;n basins and contributions from GPM in its access to the regulated transportation system in Salliquel&#243;, which on average entails about 11.7 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In 2024, the contribution of the Neuqu&#233;n Basin was 17.5% above the volume supplied the previous year, mainly due to the higher gas transportation capacity from the Vaca Muerta field facilitated by the GPM pipeline.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On the contrary, the southern basins of our country decreased their injection, as a result of the sustained natural decline of their fields and higher gas and transportation availability from the Neuqu&#233;n basin, which allowed supply alternatives to system shippers. Concerning the Austral basin, it increased its injection potential by around 2.5MMm3/d, since the start of the F&#233;nix project.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Meeting the needs of 76 direct clients and 6.2 million indirect clients, we keep playing a leading role in the natural gas industry in Argentina, rendering services either in a firm transportation (FT) or interruptible modality (IT) and ED (exchange and displacement).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The total capacity contracted by clients in a firm or <I>&#8220;take or pay&#8221;</I> modality (FT) is of 82.4 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d, with an average life term of approximately 10 years. Additionally, the system has a further capacity of 7 MMm3/d in its final sections as from the start-up of the work known as Ordoqui Loop, framed within the Transport.Ar Program set of works. The breakdown of firm contracted capacity per kind of client is represented in the graph below:</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>30</P>

<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><SUP>(1)</SUP> Information corresponding to consolidated financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><SUP>(2)</SUP> Corresponds to the ratio between the annual average deliveries and the average firm contracted capacity.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>32</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B> </B><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_26.jpg width=128 height=43 alt='Imagen 532785812' title='Imagen 532785812'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><IMG src=tgs_27.jpg width=585 height=562 alt='Imagen 532785813' title='Imagen 532785813'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_28.jpg width=316 height=159 alt='Imagen 30' title='Imagen 30'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, revenues related to this segment accounted for 46% of <B>tgs</B> total revenues. This segment&#8217;s performance went down by Ps. 21,311 million in a year-over-year comparison, from Ps. 577,973 million reported in 2023 to Ps 556,662 million in 2024.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>33</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During this year, out of the total dispatched tons, 42% of them were destined to exports. Breaking down the tons dispatched to the domestic market, 81% of them were sold at dollar-denominated prices or adjusted based on the evolution of the United States dollar.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_29.jpg width=537 height=265 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024,<B> tgs</B> ranked as the second ethane producer in Argentina, below MEGA; Our production share was of over 40% of total ethane produced in the year in our country. The graphs below show our share in aggregate propane and butane production in Argentina in 2024:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_30.jpg width=543 height=236 alt='Imagen 40' title='Imagen 40'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-28.35pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt> &nbsp;Source: Energy Secretariat and internal data</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the international reference prices of the products we export &#8212;propane, butane and natural gasoline&#8212;, they recorded slight increases of 10%, 11% and 2%, respectively. Throughout the year they showed a stable behavior, reaching their highest peak in the first quarter of the year to then stabilize and conclude the year with prices slightly higher the ones recorded in 2023, except for natural gasoline, which closed the year in a slightly downward trend.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>34</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following graph shows the evolution of average international prices from December 2023 to December 2024:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;text-indent:7.1pt;margin-left:7.1pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_31.jpg width=496 height=240 alt='Imagen 35' title='Imagen 35'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:56.7pt'><FONT style=font-size:7pt>Source: propane and butane Mont Belvieu. Natural gasoline NWEARA.</FONT><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><A name=_Hlk121334070 />In the domestic field, we keep providing propane and butane under the programs outlined by the Secretariat of Energy, under which we were granted price increases that allowed us to reverse the negative trend in the operative margins of propane and butane sold under these programs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It should be considered that in October 2024 we finalized the major maintenance of our cryogenic plant located at the Cerri Complex, which was translated into lower production in tons. This task, which is conducted every 5 years to keep performing with the highest quality and operative standards was successfully completed thanks to teamwork and the coordination of our maintenance and commercial departments.<FONT style='font:10.5pt Segoe UI;background-color:#FFFFFF'> </FONT> For further information on this topic, see &#8220;Conduction of our Operations and Safety &#8211;&nbsp;Works at Liquids Facilities&#8221;.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Foreign Market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Volumes sold overseas in 2024 amounted to 411,439 tons, thus 51,058 tons more than the 360,381 tons sold in 2023.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-7.1pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_32.jpg width=537 height=221 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'> </FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>35</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, Liquids Production and Commercialization exports revenues were Ps. 284,586 million and accounted for 24% of total revenues and 51% of total Liquids Production and Commercialization revenues.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><A name=_Hlk121334941 />In the reported year, <B>tgs </B>continued commercializing LPG by land, dispatching roughly 14,389 trucks (331,751 tons) loaded with our own product, compared to the approximately 15,518 trucks (379,544 tons) of our own product dispatched in 2023. Trucks dispatches are basically carried out to meet our domestic demand but also allow us to export our products to neighboring countries. Although their volumes are substantially lower than the exports conducted by sea, they capitalize a higher operative margin and increase our clients&#8217; portfolio.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We continued rendering logistic services at Puerto Galvan facilities in a successful manner. It is important to highlight that thanks to the high level of commitment of our people and the operative efficiency of our facilities, we could beat the daily record of the number of trucks dispatched from the Galv&#225;n Plant in the month of July 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This year, propane and butane deliveries overseas were conducted in a spot modality, seizing opportunities related to different markets&#8217; niches, which allowed us to increase considerably the fixed premiums of each transaction. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We also keep strengthening our positioning in the Brazilian market, which will delineate a 2025 goal, maintaining our sea exports in a direct modality (with no go-betweens) to Brazilian LPG distributors. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Natural gasoline exports throughout the year 2024 were conducted within the framework of an agreement entered with Trafigura Pte Ltd at an international price, less a discount, for a term of 2 years (February 2024-February 2026). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As mentioned above, generally speaking, international prices presented a stable trend in 2024, rising in the first quarter with some slight month-over-month contractions and then returning to a rising trend in the last quarter. In the short term, international prices are expected to show a rising trend.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Domestic Market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, <B>tgs</B> continued participating in the several supply programs dictated by the National Government. Such is the case of the program for LPG Supply at subsidized prices (&#8220;Household Program&#8221;)&#8212; which had been created by National Executive Decree N&#176; 470/2015. The Household Program establishes a maximum reference price to the different links of the Liquefied Petroleum Gas (&#8220;LPG&#8221;) commercialization chain to ensure its supply to low-income residential users, forcing producers to provide LPG to fractionating companies at a determined price and with a defined quota for each of them.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It is worth pointing out that on January 24, 2025, the Secretariat of Energy issued Resolution N&#176; 15/2025, which outlines that as from such date the top sale price for the products supplied under the Household Program is to be eliminated (the top sale price was the export parity issued monthly by the Secretariat of Energy under the framework of the Law N&#176; 26,020). Although said resolution maintains the obligation of LPG producers to supply the domestic market, it eliminates the product supply contributions formerly effective. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>36</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the Propane for Networks Agreement, on November 6, 2024 we entered a New Agreement for the Supply of Propane Gas to Undiluted Propane Gas Distribution Networks, in effect as from January 1, 2024 until December 31, 2024. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The terms of this agreement outlined compensations to be paid by the Argentine Government to the participants, calculated as the difference between the price at which propane is commercialized under this agreement and the export parity issued monthly by the Secretariat of Energy. However, there have been significant delays in the collection of the mentioned compensation. Its overdue balance as of December 31, 2024 was of Ps. 10,882 million. The agreement stipulates those payments shall be conducted through fiscal credit certificates to be used by the producers for the payment of hydrocarbon export rights. As of this date, the certificates corresponding to the deliveries conducted in the year 2024 have not been issued yet.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Outside the supply programs mentioned above, we sold 160,697 tons of propane and 2,828 tons of butane mainly to the fractionating market and to a lesser extent to the industrial, propellant and automobile segments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We continued commercializing ethane, under a long-term agreement entered with PBB. This agreement contemplates similar terms to the ones outlined in the previous one but involves improvements in the <I>take or pay clause </I>of annual compliance<I>,</I> which ensured us an increase in our sales volume to be implemented gradually over the first five years of the agreement. In the year 2024, ethane tons sold to PBB slightly went down to 309,894 tons, from the 394,370 tons recorded in 2023, mainly due to PBB operative difficulties throughout the year and the major maintenance we carried out at the Cerri Complex.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>37</P>

<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Natural gas processing</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_34.jpg width=361 height=491 alt='Imagen 532785825' title='Imagen 532785825'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B> </B>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>38</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Liquids Production </B></FONT>reached 1,051,654 tons (82,643 lower than in 2023). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Gas arriving to the Cerri Complex for processing slightly decreased compared to the volume recorded in 2023, mainly as a consequence of lower gas volumes arriving from the Austral basin, although by the end of the year this was reduced due to the startup of the F&#233;nix offshore reserve. Gas arriving from the Neuqu&#233;n basin presented stable volumes throughout the year but was richer, which partially offset the decrease of the Austral basin mentioned above.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Specifically, regarding product dispatches, in 2024 the number of dispatched trucks was of 29,133 (including both our own and third parties&#8217; products).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>With respect to natural gas prices denominated in dollars, which is purchased for plant PTR for Cerri Complex processing, they were lower than in 2023, measured in dollars, mainly due to the effect of the increase in natural gas supply from the Neuqu&#233;n basin.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'>(1)</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Information corresponding to consolidated financial statements</FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Midstream</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Midstream Revenues accounted for 18% of <B>tgs</B> total revenues in 2024. This segment includes the rendering of Midstream, INTEGRA and Telecommunications services (the latter rendered by the controlled company Telcosur). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Services comprised by Midstream mainly consist in gas gathering, treatment and compression services, particularly those rendered through the Vaca Muerta system and the INTEGRA services, including the operation and maintenance of third parties&#8217; facilities (pipelines and plants). </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>39</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_36.jpg width=160 height=43 alt='Imagen 27' title='Imagen 27'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_37.jpg width=549 height=246 alt='Imagen 532785814' title='Imagen 532785814'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our leadership in the natural gas industry allows us to integrate all the links of the value chain, from the wellhead to the transportation and distribution systems. We create synergies and offer differential value to our producers and clients, providing integral solutions that comprise, among others:</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>  <IMG src=tgs_38.jpg width=423 height=177 alt='Imagen 532785815' title='Imagen 532785815'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The chart below presents a breakdown of our Midstream segment revenues:</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>40</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B>Midstream revenues breakdown by service (Year 2024) </B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_39.jpg width=342 height=334 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;margin-left:85.05pt'>Source: Our own elaboration with in-company data.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Since 2018 when we set foot on Vaca Muerta, <B>tgs</B> strategy has been to be a leader in the rendering of integrated services, seeking to play a major role in the energy development of Argentina and the region. Our conditioning plant in Tratay&#233;n is a breakthrough project that contributes to energy growth.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:120.5pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_40.jpg width=289 height=286 alt='Imagen 532785809' title='Imagen 532785809'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We have already invested over US$ 700 million in <I>midstream</I> facilities to support the development carried out by natural gas producers in Vaca Muerta. With a unique project and future vision, we built a gathering pipeline network of 182 km that goes through the multiple hydrocarbon areas (Bajada de A&#241;elo, La Calera, Bandurria Sur, Fort&#237;n de Piedra, El Mangrullo, Aguada Pichana Este, Rinc&#243;n la Ceniza, Los Toldos I Sur, Pampa de las Yeguas I and II and Loma Campana) and a conditioning plant in </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>41</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Tratay&#233;n to allow access to the regulated transportation system. This conditioning plant initially had a capacity of around 5.4 MMm3/d but owing to the expansion works conducted in 2024 and the first months of 2025 will reach a total natural gas conditioning capacity of 28 MMm3/d. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The works mentioned above consisted in the installation of two Propak technology natural gas conditioning plants with a 6.6 MMm3/d capacity and involved an estimated total investment of US$ 350 million. The first plant came into service in October 2024 whereas the second plant started operations by the end of February 2025. It is noteworthy to highlight that these modular plants are adaptable to processing via a low additional investment, a first step towards the development of natural gas processing at the heart of the Neuqu&#233;n basin.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>These projects expanded Vaca Muerta System&#8217;s service rendering capacity, improving the profitability of the investment conducted by <B>tgs</B> and ensuring the availability of the infrastructure required for the production of growing natural gas volumes in Vaca Muerta, required for the exploitation of the GPM. </P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><IMG src=tgs_41.jpg width=533 height=297 alt='Imagen 45' title='Imagen 45'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:7pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>Source: data generated in-company</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Needless to say, that these investments consolidate our commitment to the development of energy in Argentina and materialize our strategy to increase the profitability of our investments in the Vaca Muerta pipeline system. For that reason, we keep in talks with different basins producers, seeking to seize business opportunities that may allow us to improve our service and enlarge our clients&#8217; portfolio.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During 2024 we launched a conceptual engineering study for the development of a project involving: (i) the expansion of the Tratay&#233;n plant that allows natural gas processing (extraction of propane, butane and natural gasoline), (ii) the building of a polyduct that facilitates the transportation of the liquids, and (iii) new fractionating, storage and dispatch facilities in Bah&#237;a Blanca. This project would address growing upstream needs as rich gases production increases.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The economic impact of this business on the region is outstanding, as it favors the development of communities and contributes to the development of the Vaca Muerta area. All the accomplishments </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>42</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>mentioned above, added to other minor services we render, emphasize, and tighten our bonds to our country&#8217;s natural gas producers and give us the chance to keep showing our talent for rendering innovating solutions.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_42.jpg width=146 height=49 alt='Imagen 13' title='Imagen 13'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_43.jpg width=311 height=342 alt='Imagen 532785844' title='Imagen 532785844'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We have made further progress in the consolidation of <B>tgs INTEGRA</B>, our business line that has the aim of integrating the different businesses and services of the energy sector. On this matter, we created a specific department to boost its growth and strengthen <B>tgs</B> as a brand within the Oil &amp; Gas segment. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This brand comprises all the services that <B>tgs</B> offers to the market relying on the human resources and tools that support the management of our own assets: from the operation and maintenance of third parties&#8217; ducts and plants, the execution of minor works and duct repairs, assistance in scrapper running, laboratory tasks such as caliber gauging and hydrocarbon samples analysis, turbine and compressors overhaul, project management, blanketing facilities services, duct integrity surveys, etc.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024 we achieved growth in this segment, being contracted for several services such as hot tap, integrity, engineering and services related to the operation and maintenance of GPM, among others. We were also hired by Golar for providing counseling services in the LNG pipeline project announced by said company in December 2024.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>43</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_44.jpg width=214 height=37 alt='Imagen 27' title='Imagen 27'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_45.jpg width=381 height=379 alt='Imagen 24' title='Imagen 24'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Now, turning to the telecommunications services rendered by Telcosur, in 2024 our controlled company closed agreements that expanded its sold capacity and consolidated its operations.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Aligned to the strategy of business consolidation both in the mid and long term, Telcosur closed agreements with new clients and extended or renewed current agreements. Among the most relevant agreements, we can mention:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Agreements with corporations</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Telcosur renewed the agreement with Oldelval for the additional term of twelve months, expanding the link and co-locations park along the route of their oil-duct. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The controlled company additionally renewed the agreement with Vista O&amp;G for the additional term of twenty- four months with park expansion in Neuqu&#233;n. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Furthermore, new clients have been added: connectivity agreements with Chevron, Pluspetrol and Tecpetrol were signed in 2024.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Agreements with Carriers</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Telcosur renewed co-locations agreement with Telecom Argentina S.A (R&#237;o Gallegos, Malaspina and Alta Barda) for the additional term of seven years. The controlled company also renewed the frame </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>44</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>and co-locations agreement in the south with AMX Argentina S.A. and the capacities agreement with Pampa del Castillo-Comodoro Rivadavia, with the addition of dark optical fiber to link the Telcosur Comodoro node. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Similarly, Telcosur expanded the AMX Argentina S.A. LTE networks transportation capacities agreement with producers in the Neuqu&#233;n basin fields.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Installation of an optical fiber network in Vaca Muerta</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, Telcosur completed the works related to the linking of the high-capacity optical fiber network from Northern Vaca Muerta up to Sierra Chata (Pampa Energ&#237;a), Bajada de A&#241;elo (Shell), El Trapial (Chevron), Los Toldos (Pluspetrol), Tratayen - Sierras Blancas (Shell) to provide telecommunication services to oil and gas producers in Vaca Muerta, who require large-volume data transmission for their operations and communications. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, two new fiber optic links were made to Tratay&#233;n Plant:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>1) From our node located in the city of Neuqu&#233;n to the node located in Tratay&#233;n.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>2) From the Tratay&#233;n Plant owned by Transportadora de Gas del Sur S.A. to the Plant of Compa&#241;&#237;a MEGA S.A. located in Neuqu&#233;n.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This allowed Telcosur to provide redundancy and high-capacity availability over the optical fiber in Vaca Muerta, providing point-to-point or multipoint solutions at constant speed, high capacity and Carrier-Class quality which, for its voice, data, internet and video integration features, leads to an optimization of costs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>New technologies</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B> </B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Telcosur is not detached from the digital transformation process we are going through. Performing in a sector where technological advance and innovation prevail, our controlled company has been applying new technologies that introduce artificial intelligence and remote operation of facilities. This has led to the installation of predictive remote monitoring systems that implemented mechanisms of early detection of events in networks and generated proactive action in benefit of customer service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Furthermore, Telcosur implemented a development of digital twins and 3D model for aerial support masts. With the application of an innovating technology, a system was implemented for monitoring the verticalization of aerial support structures as preventive support to the maintenance of these critical mission systems.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Telcosur worked on the definition of a new VMS Milestone technology to manage cameras systems at the tele- commanded plants, optimizing bandwidth for them and offering scheduled and AI alerts that are managed from the control center. </P>
