<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>5. Conduction of Operations and Safety</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style=font-size:10pt;color:#00B0F0><B>Pipeline system operated by tgs in Argentina</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_47.jpg width=405 height=649 alt='Imagen 532785816' title='Imagen 532785816'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>47</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Pipeline and Compressor Plants Investment Works</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The gas pipeline system operated by <B>tgs</B> adequately met both the natural gas transportation demand and the regulatory entity&#8217;s requirements. An efficient management of the pipeline system entails the performance of assessment, maintenance, inspection, mitigation and prevention tasks to avoid failures that may have a negative impact, guaranteeing service continuity without future interruptions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Throughout the 2024, we conducted integrity assessment tasks, such as in-line inspections along 907 km of pipelines and pipelines evaluations through direct assessment techniques, namely CIS and DCVG surveys over 192 km (on sections with no Scraper Traps). The purpose of these tasks was to identify, assess and control threats to pipelines integrity, such as external corrosion, geometrical defects, manufacture and/or construction anomalies, recoating failures, among others.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding our recoating program, in 2024 we executed recoating replacements over 6 km of the pipeline system. We are also carrying out recoating works at three valve facilities along the San Martin Pipeline in the province of Buenos Aires.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In these works we have introduced non&#8211;destructive tests by means of the use of magnetic particles, ultrasound and hardness measurements for the identification of anomalies in longitudinal weld seams, stress corrosion cracking (&#8220;SCC&#8221;), by means of which we were able to detect anomalies that could be mitigated through pipe replacements. These recoating works aim at mitigating the risk of external corrosion and/or SCC as well as at surveying and correcting any pipe anomaly that in the future may deteriorate the ducts and interrupt the service. This is how we extend the useful life of our facilities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Along these lines, in order to mitigate corrosive activity, we carried out reinforcement sleeves filling works to ensure integrity in high population density areas.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As part of the SCC assessment and mitigation plan, we implemented an inline inspection plan with &#8220;EMAT&#8221; technology to detect cracking, having completed the inspection of 155 km in 2024. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Based on an analysis and planning carried out by the pipeline integrity team, we conducted an assessment campaign of 186 external corrosion and geometrical defects, anomalies in longitudinal and circumferential seams, which required immediate repairs that were executed by means of the installation of 3 pipe replacements and 5 welding reinforcements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Furthermore, we assessed the integrity of 20 derivations located in pipeline sections in the Province of Buenos Aires.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Concerning cathodic protection, to mitigate the advance of corrosion and improve reliability, we kept strengthening the system with the installation of 5 new units and 16 current reinforcement lenses as well as the renewal of 12 facilities that were obsolete. We also installed batteries anodes at two high-voltage lines crossings.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Besides, in 2024 we conducted adaptations of the cathodic protection systems at compressor plants, installing 2 lens reinforcements and conducted special protection level surveys at 4 compressor plants. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>48</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We also surveyed potentials and conducted bathymetric studies in two sections of offshore pipelines owned by <B>tgs</B>. This survey is carried out every three years and the resulting information is key to monitor cathodic protection levels and find out whether the positioning of the ducts has been affected by sea currents movements. This survey was also conducted in 4 segments of pipelines crossing several rivers. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>All the works mentioned above&#8212; which enable us to verify the overall condition of the facility and conduct the required adjustments for their service continuity&#8212; underline our commitment to safety and to the care of our people and the environment, all main cornerstones in our pledge to render a reliable natural gas transportation service. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Being the Buenos Aires ring a high population density area and driven by our goal to minimize inherent risks in such pipeline segment, we continued with the implementation of works that mitigate third parties&#8217; damage. Thus, we went ahead with the expansion of the optical fiber intruder detection system in further 24.5 km of pipeline, in the area of Las Heras and Ca&#241;uelas. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Similarly, in order to raise awareness among the community, we increased our facilities&#8217; signaling and &#8212;in the areas neighboring the most sensitive sections of the system&#8212; we conducted virtual and remote damage prevention activities, such as communications via radio, media and networks for the public in general, as well as meetings with municipalities and ground-breaking &amp; services companies in Buenos Aires, Neuqu&#233;n and Rio Negro. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>One of the key landmarks to be highlighted regarding the operation of the transportation system is the startup of the Tratay&#233;n and Salliquel&#243; compressor plants, corresponding to the GPM, which reorganized operative planning due to the possible access of up to 21 MMm3/d into the final sections of the <B>tgs </B>transportation system<B>.</B> Besides, the Mercedes compressor plant &#8212;head plant of the Mercedes&#8211;Cardales Pipeline&#8212;came into service, enabling an increase of deliveries to TGN.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding technological changes, we concluded the integration of the Saturno and Olavarria compressor plants with the CABA Operative center, thus taking the first steps in the development of our new philosophy related to the remote operation of our transportation system. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the Belisle compressor plant, we completed the Detailed Engineering and Factory Acceptance Test (FAT) of the dashboard corresponding to the Plant Control System, the installation of which will be carried out in the year 2025. This development will allow us to integrate such facility to the CABA Operative Center.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the Gaviotas Compressor Plant, we developed the Extended Detailed Engineering for the updating of the plant control system and the electricity generation system, works that will be carried out over the years 2025 and 2026.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>With respect to turbine control systems, we conducted the updating work of the turbine compressor N&#176;2 Solar Mars of the Cervantes Compressor Plant and moved forward with the updating works of the turbine compressor N&#176;2 Hispano Suiza 1203 of the Bosque Petrificado Compressor Plant, foreseen to be concluded in the first quarter of 2025.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>49</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Additionally, we purchased and supplied the control dashboards of the three turbine compressor units of the Gaviotas Compressor Plant, which are to be installed during the years 2025 and 2026, for their integration with the technological update of the plant control system and electricity generation mentioned above.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the systems that monitor the vibration of turbine compressor units, we have updated and integrated them with the centralized monitoring system of Bentley Nevada at the Belisle, Indio Rico, Saturno, Manantiales Behr and Ordoqui Compressor Plants.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the other hand, in connection with the operative safety of the compressor facilities, we purchased all the required material and made progress with the works related to the updating of the detection of Fire&amp;Gas at the Belisle, Bajo el Gualicho and Dolavon Compressor Plants, the startup of which is scheduled for the first quarter of 2025. Along the same lines, we are developing the extended basic engineering for the updating of the Saturno, Chelfor&#243; and La Adela Compressor Plants, due to the technological obsolescence of the current systems and the need to ensure their reliability, in view of the transportation system remote operation philosophy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Within our environmental care activities, we assembled the biodigester systems at the Belisle, Cervantes and Chelfor&#243; plants to improve their current sewage effluent systems. We further acquired the materials required to develop similar works at the remaining compressor plants in &nbsp;2025 and 2026.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Under <B>tgs</B> Emissions Reduction Plan, in 2024 we developed a campaign to detect and quantify natural gas leakage emissions at 20 compressor plants, with the objective to prioritize needs and devise an adequate plan to address, repair and eliminate said leakages.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Works at Liquids Facilities</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>We conducted works related to the major maintenance plan and extension of the useful life of pressure containers and ducts at the Cerri Complex, to boost their reliability, reduce risks at facilities and meet government requirements. These tasks included several unit inspections, the execution of corrective and preventive maintenance at our facilities, along with the tuning of the Cryogenic Plant control room and other adjustments required to eliminate bottleneck at the fractionating trains. We also included the inspection and maintenance of the venting system and related equipment. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>On the other hand, we conducted the Puerto Galv&#225;n cooling plant maintenance, conducting both preventive and corrective maintenance tasks both in equipment and ducts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Focused on ensuring the continuous and safe management of our facilities, we certified spheres 8 and 10 at the Galv&#225;n Plant, the separators of the steam compression system and the settlement tank. We adjusted the V1 tank and assembled the new pumps for the cooling of the compressors&#8217; oil, increasing the reliability of the Cooling Plant refrigeration system.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the other hand, we started investments to mitigate technological obsolescence<FONT style=font-family:Arial;color:#0C0C0C;background-color:#FFFCCF> </FONT>risks and increase our processes&#8217; operative safety. We can highlight the following developments: installation of a new electricity distribution transformer at the Galv&#225;n Plant, a new Safety Instrumented System at boilers </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>50</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>and cryogenic systems, new medium-voltage cells at the Cryogenic Plant and revamping of the excitation generators control system.</FONT></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Works at Vaca Muerta Facilities</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Tratay&#233;n plant expansion is developed through the projects required to increase the supply of Midstream services. This year, we reached a major landmark in terms of the expansion capacity of the Tratay&#233;n conditioning plant, starting up the first gas conditioning &#8220;Propak&#8221; module in October 2024. This module, which may be adapted in the future to the processing of gathered natural gas, allow us to increase capacity by 6.6 MMm3/d. The second Propak module came on operations at the end of February 2025, increasing total plant capacity to 28 MMm3/d.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Focused on the growth of the Vaca Muerta system, in 2024 we pursued the works required to launch other auxiliary services such as a new gasoline stabilization plant (the third for this facility), the expansion of the systems for natural gas inflows and outflows, new offices and <I>dormies</I> and the construction of a new gasoline-duct that will enable the dispatch of the produced gasoline, to address daily production increases and minimize truck transit.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Specifically, on the Vaca Muerta pipeline, we made progress in the hydric risk mitigation works. Works were carried out from the progressive PK 0, located at the Tratay&#233;n conditioning plant up to the PK 115, in the area of Los Toldos I Sur. Besides, in the VMS, we conducted works from the PK 0, in the area Aguada Pichana Este, up to the PK 32, in Bandurria Sur. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Throughout the year 2024, we worked on the development of applications for the software of Vaca Muerta System- Tratay&#233;n Dispatch System. We completed the development of the modules of maintenance and programming, performing user tests and taking functionalities to production mode. We will continue working on the modules of allocation, invoicing, report generator and auditor, with the aim of unifying all the processes in this platform. Additionally, given the growth in operations, we conducted investment to address emergencies.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Lastly, <B>tgs</B> participated in several works for third parties, putting into service facilities such as the links of Pampa Energ&#237;a, Pluspetrol and Vista to the Vaca Muerta System.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;color:#0070C0'><B>6. Environment, Social and Governance Matters</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At <B>tgs</B> we pledge to run our businesses and operate our facilities in compliance with the requirements of applicable legislation and the obligations to which we willingly adhere. We strive to meet our customers&#8217; expectations, prioritizing our service quality, pollution prevention and our staff and contractors&#8217; safety and health through the continuous improvement of our management system&#8217;s efficiency. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In view of that, in 2024 we kept working on our Environmental, Social, Governance Plan (the &#8220;ESG Plan&#8221;), prioritizing these three main topics and action lines that will drive our purpose on this matter. Having completed the overall diagnosis of our ESG management in 2023, in the reported year we consolidated our commitment by means of a double materiality analysis, aligning our ESG strategy </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>51</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>and our corporate vision. This process allowed us to detect impacts, risks and opportunities (&#8220;IROs&#8221;) relevant to sustainability and financial performance, bearing in mind both the market trends and the demands of our stakeholder groups.&#160;</FONT></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-a) ENVIRONMENT</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>The year 2024 found us half way in our path to implement our environmental strategic plan (2022-2026), which integrates environment with all phases of business processes, from strategic decision-making to risk and opportunities management, planning, design and execution of activities. The plan comprises the following strategic axes, plans and action programs:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>1.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Mitigation and adaptation to climate change</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Our strategic environmental plan outlines a <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>50%</B> <B>reduction goal</B> </FONT>for our methane emissions by the year 2030 (departing from emissions recorded in the year 2021).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Driven towards such goal, we launched a comprehensive emissions action plan, which comprises investments, improvements and good practices required to fulfil it. The plan included the following actions:</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Enhance our emissions inventory</B></FONT>: the gap analysis we conducted in 2023 versus ISO 14064-1: 2018 Standard (Carbon Footprint Management System) led us to devise a plan to implement the arising recommendations, for which purpose in 2024 we issued an internal procedure to manage and control our inventory of greenhouse effect gases emission to the atmosphere (GHG), either directly or indirectly generated by <B>tgs activities</B>. Besides, we conducted a review of our emissions inventory, including non-relevant emissions as well, adjusting chromatography and the global warming potential factors, based on the latest report of the Government Group of Experts on Weather Change (&#8220;IPCC&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>We kept moving forward in our plan to record <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Scope 3 emissions </B></FONT>in our emissions inventory (emissions derived from our supply chain and that are not under our control), based on our ranking of potential sources of indirect emissions that we studied in 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Preparation of the Overall Investments Plan:</B></FONT> within the framework of the Five-Year Tariff Review 2025-2029, we developed a five-year overall investments plan in which one of the main cornerstones is the reduction of emissions, through the technological updating of facilities and the permanent search for new technologies. The plan is focused on the purchase of devices and systems that allow us to efficiently detect, reduce and &nbsp;measure the gas emission sources at our facilities in the natural gas transportation segment. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>Besides, we conducted a review of our internal procedures in emissions management, focused on one of our facilities (the Garayalde Compressor Plant) which seeks to standardize the facility venting estimation and measurement and extrapolate it to the other compressor plants through internal trainings.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>52</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Climate Risk assessment:</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B> </B></FONT>In 2024 we conducted a survey to identify <B>tgs climate risks</B>, thus beginning to address this issue of global relevance. In this first stage, we assessed the climate risks that our company faces, and in the 2025 we will continue with the devising of the strategy to manage said risks, through adaptation and business continuity.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>As part of said process, we have consolidated a document with the historical record of extreme climate changes that impacted on our facilities. This tool allows us to minimize uncertainties related to the likelihood of occurrence of extreme weather events. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>For the assessment of climate risks, we used weather projection models (Climate Change Risk Maps System 3 (SIMARCC) &#8211;&nbsp;RCP 4.5 Near Future Scenario 2015-2039), following the recommendations of the Argentina Climate Change department. We identified climate threats for future scenarios and through a scientific based methodology, we conducted a qualitative appraisal of the climate risk of our facilities. With the results of the climate threat matrix per facility, we will consolidate our climate resilience strengthening plan, including preventive measures as well as response and recovery plans in the face of extreme weather events. &nbsp;This will reduce our vulnerability to the identified risks, protecting both our people and our infrastructure and ensuring business continuity. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Energy Management</B><B>: </B></FONT>We are energy that moves forward, so at <B>tgs</B> we are genuinely interested in the efficient use of energy. Part of the natural gas we transport is used as energy for our compressors and combustion processes in the transportation business. &nbsp;Thus, we must be efficient in the use of this non-renewable natural resource. Moreover, we use it as fuel for generators of electric engines that produce electricity. At some facilities, we complete self-generation with electricity from external sources. We have meters and are aware of our consumption. In our energy matrix we can visualize our consumption and understand trends to identify opportunities for improvement.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>In the reported year, we conducted a gap analysis of this matter against the requirements of Standard ISO 50001:2018 (Energy Management System) in order to implement an energy management system for <B>tgs</B>. With this report we can define the initial status with reference to above-mentioned standard, to then devise the action plan and work on the measures required for its implementation in the oncoming years. We held company meetings to determine the processes required to comply with each of the provisions of the standard. We have thus taken the first steps in this direction, convinced of the importance of having an energy management system and improving our energy performance. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>Fully aware of the relevance of generating cooperative opportunities among companies and universities that contribute with knowledge and innovating ideas, which opens the door to new solutions, in &nbsp;2024 we worked with the National University of the Center of the Province of Buenos Aires (UNICEN, for its acronym in Spanish) to conduct a thorough energy review, following the guidelines and requirements provided by Standard ISO 50001, for the Olavarr&#237;a Compressor Plant, located in the province of Buenos Aires. With this report we gathered </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>53</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>accurate data related to consumption and could identify required improvement and actions on which we must focus in this process.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Biodiversity and Foresting Management</B><B>: </B></FONT>in 2023<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>we implemented our Policy of Foresting and Biodiversity, thus showing our commitment to these issues of such relevance in environmental care.&#160;By 2024, 100% of our facilities had completed their foresting baseline studies, so that they can now go ahead with the outlining of their foresting plan. The purpose of this plan, besides the generation of benefits such as carbon sequestration, is to improve the biodiversity of the site, the nutrients cycle, soil, the hydric dynamics of the premises, climate protection and landscaping. Besides, in 2024 we issued an internal procedure to manage biodiversity and forestation, outlining the basic concepts to approach this matter.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>As a management tool, at <B>tgs</B> we identified the existing protected areas in the provinces where we operate, in order to monitor them and promote the conservation of natural ecosystems. For such purposes, we moved forward with the creation of an app within <B>tgs </B>geographical information system, called &#8220;Protected areas environmental map&#8221;, where we can visualize protected areas (both public and private), areas protected through ordinances of native forest and biosphere reserves over which our facilities (ducts, valves, cathodic protection units, measurement points, scraper traps, compressor plants and operative bases) have or might have influence due to overlapping or closeness. Thus, this information is available for the whole company.&#160;&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>We also added the areas inhabited by the species that are part of the UICN Red List and national lists of preservation for risk of extinction and that match our operation areas.&#160;&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt'>This information will help us identify where our activities pose a threat for plants and animals at risk of extinction. The identification of such threats will allow us to take the required steps to avoid damage and prevent species extinction.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B>2.</B></FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B>Proactive environmental leadership</B></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We have followed up facilities leaders&#8217; performance on these matters so that they continue deepening the roles they play in environmental management. Besides, we conducted training activities related to the environmental strategic plan, to communicate it and drive action. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In the reported year, we launched the Sustainable Mindset Program targeted at the high and middle management of our company, in charge of professionals from the Di Tella University. Its aim was to strengthen the skills of our leaders so that they can make the best decisions while protecting our planet. We plan to further pursue this program in 2025, as the experience was very positive and generated valuable contributions for our organization.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>As every year, we conducted environmental awareness programs that relate to special environmental celebrations or events, which included talks on topics of interest, trivia and information on several undertaken initiatives.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>54</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>3.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Circular processes and consumption</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B><I>Water resources management</I></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In our Environmental Strategic Plan (2022-2026), we outlined the goal to recover 80% of sewage effluents for irrigation by the year 2025. <B>In 2024 we completed the purchase of biodigesters for the treatment of sanitary effluents</B>. Currently, at 10 of our plants biodigesters are operative whereas we have already bought the ones required for the remaining 22, which are planned to be installed between 2025 and 2026.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Besides, in 2023 we obtained the quantification of our direct water footprint to achieve a more efficient management of this limited natural resource. This indicator is a first step in the achievement of efficient improvements in water management and awareness of our water consumption and use. In 2024 we continued improving the indicator through a more specific approach at each facility.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In February 2024, we decided to annul the connection that enabled the dumping of our treated sewage effluents into the Bah&#237;a Blanca estuary. Since then, the Galv&#225;n storage plant is a zero-effluents facility, landmark that shows the commitment of the people who daily conduct the treatment and our corporate commitment to a matter of such a relevance as the management of water resources.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This action is even more relevant given where the Galv&#225;n Plant is located, being an area of a significant water deficit, and where water supply is conducted via an aqueduct from Bahia Blanca, a city almost 60 kilometers away.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B><I>Waste Management</I></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Driven by the goal outlined in our ESG Strategic plan to achieve a recycling rate over 50% by 2026, we carried out several actions to minimize generated waste. &nbsp;We conducted several awareness campaigns and training activities that encouraged steps towards a paperless office, reduction of plastic items of only one use, hiring of new waste operators, disposal of electric devices in disuse, among others.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Additionally, in the reported year, we conducted waste workshops per provinces, addressing existing waste management issues in accordance with regulations in force, summoning stakeholders to the facilities. We analyzed the most relevant issues addressed by regulations and dealt with doubts and enquiries. We also proposed improvements, based on the needs identified at each facility. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Furthermore, in April 2024 we held an awareness talk led by the cooperative Bera Recicla, of the city of Berazategui, where the Guti&#233;rrez complex is located. As a further key benefit derived from this initiative, we could get the mentioned organization to manage all inert waste (recyclable) at present, thus promoting a responsible management of waste and contributing to a circular economy. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>55</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-b) SOCIAL</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>HUMAN CAPITAL</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We closed a year of growth that witnessed great achievements and challenges.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In the field of talent attraction, we <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>developed an employer brand survey that allowed us to design specific actions for in-company and external positioning</B></FONT>. We were present at several institutional events, such as the Oil &amp; Gas Exhibition in Neuqu&#233;n and in the segment Oil &amp; Gas Youth, with great success and attendance. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We also participated at employment fairs of universities such as UBA, UTN, UNS, among others, with the aim of sharing with them our brand and value proposal. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Pursuing our goal of developing a sustainable business, we place a strong focus on our neighboring communities, generating local employment. To that end, we participated in supervised professional practices for high schools, aiming at strengthening the bond between companies and educational institutions. We also conduct employability workshops that target the students of the last years of technical schools in the provinces of Buenos Aires and Neuqu&#233;n, to provide them with tools to get higher quality jobs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#0070C0'><B>We play a leading role in our country economic growth and we know our people are key to our success.</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We build a healthy and challenging work environment and we watch over the integral wellbeing of our people. <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>For over ten years, we have been one of the greatest places to work in Argentina, as certified by the global firm Great Place to Work</B></FONT>. We accordingly support all our areas with integration and work climate activities that favor bonds and closeness among the members of the several departments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In the reported year&#8217;s fast-paced and challenging rhythm, we were able to close <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>103 selection processes, including (84) new employees&#8217; recruitment as well as (19) internal promotions</B></FONT> <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>opportunities. </B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We kept promoting <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>our Ambassadors Program, the aim of which is to build a network of transforming agents who can communicate the desired culture to fulfil our dream.</B></FONT> This group of people undertakes initiatives that make them leaders in <B>tgs</B> culture and communication, representing our values and the cornerstones of an open, dynamic and cooperative management.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Learning at tgs </B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We take pride in our accomplishments. Learning empowers our strategy and is a key factor to build knowledge and organizational capacities. For this reason, we decided to go far beyond designing a simple online courses platform and thus gave birth to <B>tgs</B> Campus, our learning ecosystem. <B>Tgs </B>Campus has 1,147 active students and currently it hosts 254 available contents that address different topics: management, leadership, businesses, human competences, techniques, etc. Moreover, we have 123 of our own contents prepared by our workers, included in our academies.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>56</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Within the framework of our open, dynamic and cooperative learning strategy, this year we developed <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>the technical schools, </B></FONT>with<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>specializations<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> in Anticorrosive Protection, Gas Turbines, Compressors, Gas Quality, Earth Connection Values Measurement. PLC Level 1</B> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As a result of this project, we have consolidated a team of in-company instructors deeply engaged and committed to the development of knowledge at <B>tgs</B>.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Growing and leading at tgs</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At <B>tgs</B> we promote the culture of empowerment, in which every person plays the leading role in his/her own performance. &nbsp;Through a cycle of planning, follow-up and assessment, each worker proposes objectives aligned to the business management, plans his/her own continuous improvement, assesses his/her own results and conduct and thinks along with his/her supervisors about his/her learning. Thus, performance processes provide information for decision-making with respect to learning, development and compensation. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Similarly, we <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>set up Talent Committees</B></FONT> to survey our workers&#8217; current and projected capacity and assess their potential to take on positions of greater complexity in the future. Thanks to this process and through a detailed analysis of competences, we identify the potential future leaders of the organization, manage talent and conduct a strategic planning regarding the needs of work teams and our business. This facilitates decision-making on the topic of promotions, transfers, turnovers, project team and replacement charts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The result of this work provides us with <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>talent matrixes for all the departments</B></FONT> and complete <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>sustainability maps</B></FONT> for leadership positions in our company. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>With our eyes set on future development, <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>we have conducted development talks with leaders and talent, </B></FONT>process that guarantees talent and business sustainability at <B>tgs</B>, to be able to have human capital who is prepared and available for the future of the business and the organization. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=color:#000000>We also continued working with our leaders, through the </FONT><B>&#8220;On-Mode leadership&#8221; in its edition &#8220;rock your leadership at tgs&#8221;. </B><FONT style=color:#000000>We held presence-based workshops, three training days with outsourced speakers, and an immersive week of online learning.</FONT><B> </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Focused on learning from others and adopting best practices, in cooperation with <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Voces Vitales Cono Sur </B></FONT>and the<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> Argentinian Institute of Oil &amp; Gas, we renewed our participation in the Mentoring Program,</B></FONT> an initiative designed to empower the role of women in leadership positions within the industry. This program contemplates three training days in soft skills, complemented by two days of direct learning along the CEO or an officer from another company appointed as mentor.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Culture and transformation at tgs</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We keep evolving and getting close to our target culture. This year we <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>redesigned our organizational behaviors </B></FONT>to give them more visibility, closeness and impact and be able to align them to the new business challenges. <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>I innovate cooperatively, I place the client at the core, I </B></FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>57</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>am part and I go for more</B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>, are our cornerstones and focus when pursuing our objectives and business challenges.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We also take pride in <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>having set up the change management team this year,</B> </FONT>which is integrated by employees from many departments, in charge of carrying out actions related to digital transformation and business strategies.</P>

<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Diversity and inclusion</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Social changes gave rise to the need of addressing diversity, inclusion and spaces free from violence. This year our purpose was to focus on the training and sensitization of our whole staff.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>After setting up the Committee of Diversity and Inclusion, we developed and communicated our principles on diversity, equity and inclusion, to act as guidelines and delineate our purpose and scope.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We carried out several workshops: the ABC of diversity, biases, age diversity, disability and respect at the workplace. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We developed 3 e-learning capsules on the ABC of diversity, biases, workspace free from violence and discrimination.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>58</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We implemented a calendar of special occasions or commemoration days that reminded us of different events and invited us to think.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We developed a manual on inclusive recruitment, a communication channel with neutral gender language and a protocol on how to act in case of reports on work violence and/or harassment </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We participated in the IAPG Diversity, Equity and Inclusion Commission with concrete action: development of the recommended practices in terms of DEI, which seek to align all the industry companies; and on the other hand we participated in the Voces Vitales Program on mentoring for industry leading women.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>All these actions drive us forward towards organizational changes that generate actual inclusion.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>This approach to the topic of diversity and inclusion started with an overall diagnosis conducted in the year 2022 along with an action plan focused on the development of a workspace that is respectful, inclusive and free from violence.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>As we included in our ESG Plan, we are conducting actions that turn us into a more diverse company, for instance: promotion of women to positions usually filled by men such as heads of shift, gas dispatch operators, plant leaders and truck loading operators.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Relations with Unions</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Our 2024 calendar comprised many meetings with union representatives. The agenda of such talks and the reached agreements contemplated inflation rates variations observed in the country. Monthly collective bargaining migrated to less regular increases as inflation indexes started to go down. The good work climate created by both parties promoted a sincere dialogue and materialized into collective bargaining agreements that are essential to ensure the continuity and safety of operations. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We have consolidated the work of the Mixed Commission of Safety, Environment and Occupational Health, integrated by the representatives of all the unions that represent <B>tgs </B>employees, whose purpose is to strengthen a risk prevention behavior so that safe working conditions are ensured, specifically focusing on the care of the psycho- physical health and optimizing risk prevention for health, hygiene and safety at work. This commission holds regular meetings in which matters within their areas of responsibility are addressed. As part of their work, this year the commission visited the facilities of Dolavon Compressor Plant and Operative Base, both located in the province of Chubut. During said visit, the members of the commission could learn about and oversee all the operations conducted by <B>tgs</B> at such operative facilities and talk with the workforce of these sectors regarding the scope of their tasks and issues related to safety and health.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>59</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Occupational Health</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Taking care of the safety and health of our people is one of the cornerstones of our performance. &nbsp;Bearing in mind that accidents related to upper limbs accounted for 38% of recorded labor accidents over the last 20 years, in 2024 we conducted several activities to raise awareness and prevent them.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Another 2024 outstanding initiative was the recording of health checkups and controls for our own employees or contractors&#8217; staff. To such purposes we have upgraded the functions of the in-company tool used for health self-management. This year we added the function &#8220;medical fitness for the role&#8221;, which allows to update the staff information as health checkups are performed at the different facilities. Besides helping us to manage the health of each worker, this tool generates personalized recommendations and allows us to assess if our workers are fit to perform tasks such as: driving heavy or light vehicles, height jobs, confined spaces or perform as brigade volunteers.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We inaugurated a new space at the Galv&#225;n and Tratay&#233;n Plants to perform preventive health checkups to hazardous loads drivers, targeting all the drivers who daily load products at such facility. This measure allows us to make progress in accidents prevention and in the detection of any anomaly that may impact on their health.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The reported year we obtained the recertification in the program directed by SEDRONAR &#8212;the Secretariat of Comprehensive Policies on Drugs&#8212; as preventive work environments. This certification audits the companies that have an addiction prevention policy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Lastly, we were awarded the first prize to the best scientific poster &#8220;DETECTION, ANALYISS AND APPROACH TO THE IMPACT OF PSYCHOSOCIAL IMPACT ON TGS PERSONNEL DURING AND POST PANDEMIC&#8221;, presented before the 26<SUP>th</SUP> Labor Medicine Congress of the Province of Buenos Aires, SMTBA for its acronym in Spanish. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>&#8220;+Energy Program&#8221; </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>In the year 2019 we created the acknowledgement program known as </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>&#8220;+Energy&#8221;, </B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>which underlines and acknowledges the achievements of our workers who transmit <B>tgs</B> culture through their individual, team work and/or their commitment with the organization. Throughout the reported year, we acknowledged: &#160;</FONT></P>

<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B>workers for their outstanding actions</B></P>
