<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Benefits Program</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>tgs </B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>offers an outstanding and very competitive benefits package: &#8220;</FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#4F81BD'><B>Plus!&#8221;, </B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>designed to address the needs of all our staff. Organized by segments, the program offers creative and specific options that consider family, health promotion and care, recreation, loans and insurance. These benefits ensure a flexible and comfortable work environment and have been devised to support all our employees and their families in every life stage. </FONT></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Calibri;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:8pt;color:#242424'><FONT style='font:10pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>SAFETY AND HYGIENE</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Safety is one of the values that stands out in our performance. Focused on prevention and risk minimization, we strive to generate commitment to a self-care culture in safety. We understand Occupational Safety and Health as a consequence of the engagement of all the persons who are part of our company and interact with us, so its scope reaches our own staff, third parties&#8217; personnel, suppliers and the community on the whole. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our 2022-2026 Safety and Hygiene Strategic Plan approved by the Management Committee. It revolves around three strategic axes: </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt'><A name=_Hlk158298025 /><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'>1.</KBD>Preventive Leadership Culture for an Interdependent Organization that is based on self-care and care among all the members of each team (&#8220;I take care of myself and I take care of my mate&#8221;) &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'>2.</KBD>Risk-based industrial processes for high reliability facilities. This involves an efficient management of resources based on facilities risks (reliability in the life cycle)&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'>3.</KBD>An organization prepared for contingencies, focused on loss minimization and mitigation towards business continuity in the face of contingencies (preparation, response and rehabilitation). &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>This plan reaches and engages all organization levels: processes, stakeholders and workers. We thus ensure safe and healthy work conditions throughout the whole production chain. Year after year, we prepare a matrix with critical tasks that involve the continuity of some initiatives and the addition of others with the objective to keep consolidating a sustainable safety culture.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In 2024 we designed a program known as CICLO SOL+ in all company areas. The purpose of this program is to maintain organization, order and cleaning conditions at the work space, enhance the comfort, safety and ergonomics of work spaces and improve work environment on the whole. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>We implemented the <I>Bowtie</I> risk management methodology, which visually shows the relations among risks, threats and controls by means of a graph to avoid the deterioration of the preventive and mitigation measures. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#242424>In 2024 we relaunched a campaign on safety, in order to consolidate and keep deepening leadership in the safety culture. To that aim, we conducted several activities such as: safety spots with different topics, activities in the week dedicated to Safety within the frame of the National Day of Hygiene and</FONT> </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>61</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Safety, meeting spaces at the facilities to discuss Safety, Environment, and Quality, multiple communications by mail, among others.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding our involvement with stakeholders and interested parties, we have participated in the Energy Sector Innovation Table, summoned by the Labor Risk Superintendency, seeking to develop joint action to monitor and strengthen risk prevention at the company with 4.0 technology. Furthermore, in 2024 we participated again of the recertification to Preventive Labor Environments, along with SEDRONAR. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In the reported year, we also consolidated continuity in brigade training, which we will keep working and deepening in the upcoming years. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>SEQ Culture program as value&#160;</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Segoe UI;margin:0'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#7030A0'>&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#000000'>The program Safety, Environment and Quality Culture (SEQ or &#8220;SAC&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish) as Value is a key cornerstone within our Labor Risk Prevention Programs. This initiative is part of the axis &#8220;Preventive Leadership Culture for an Interdependent Organization&#8221; within <B>tgs&#8217;</B> Safety and Hygiene Strategic Plan.&#160;&#160;This program seeks to consolidate good practices in terms of safety and health that can improve our performance. In this line, we have a platform on health and safety at work for our employees, raising awareness in the area and boosting commitment to it. Among these training programs, we may mention </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'><B>Safe and Sustainable Vehicles Driving Program </B></FONT><FONT style=color:#000000>and</FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'><B> Brigade Volunteers Training Program</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#000000><B>.</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>The scope of our commitment to safety and hygiene reaches our contractors. Therefore, we implemented initiatives such as the standardization of health and safety requirements, an interactive training system and the creation of an Assessment Matrix. We have also strengthened communication through regular meetings and we have introduced updates, such as a work permits system. Our strategy aims at compliance with safety standards, continuous improvement and adaptation to current technologies, showing our commitment to safety, quality and sustainability in projects, with the engagement of third parties.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>The communication of relevant information related to hygiene and safety at work is regularly conducted through several channels:&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>By means of a performance report that informs safety, vehicle and environmental incidents, accumulated rates, indexes.&#160;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>We have set up a Hygiene and Safety Joint Committee in which both company and workers representatives participate to promote risk prevention and keep safe working conditions. It also seeks to protect workers&#8217; life and health and improve labor conditions. The Committee members have different roles, such as chairman, secretary, company and workers representatives, among others. The ethics of an interdependent care to promote our corporate safety culture is underscored. The social representation of the workers is in line with the current legislations and the participation of all persons involved in safety and hygiene issues &nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>62</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>is encouraged by way of varied communication strategies. In 2024, we consolidated the relationship channels in the Safety and Health Joint Committee, conducting presentations and communicating important matters in the consolidation of our desired culture.&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Quality Management System</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our Safety, Health, Environment and Quality Policy outlines the commitment of the Management on these matters. It is the reference on which the Integrated Management System is based (SGI) in accordance with ISO 45001:2018, ISO 14001:2015 and ISO 9001:2015 and is aligned to the organizational dream or challenge and the desired culture.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Policy is posted at the company website: <FONT style='color:#0000FF;border-bottom:1px solid #0000FF'>https://www.tgs.com.ar/our-company/environment</FONT>. <B>tgs </B>has Environment, Safety and Quality Strategic Plans to ensure the fulfilment of commitments undertaken in the Safety, Health, Environment and Quality Policy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><FONT style=color:#000000>At&#160;<B>tgs,</B>&#160;we carry out our processes in accordance with the following principles:</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Management of the context and stakeholders&#8217; due diligence to determine and address emerging risks and opportunities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Identification of hazards and risk control to prevent injuries and provide safe and healthy work conditions.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Environmental protection, including pollution prevention and minimization of environmental impacts derived from our activities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Fulfilment of our clients&#8217; needs and expectations, prioritizing the quality of our products and services, competitiveness and profitability.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Compliance with legal and other requirements.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Clear and transparent communication that facilitates channels for queries and the participation of every worker at all company levels.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Culture of achievement, through the management of goals and indicators.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Development and growth of our employees, promoting improvement in their competences and raising of their awareness.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In 2024, we successfully passed the Recertification Audit of ISO 14001:2015 / ISO 9001:2015 and ISO 45001:2018. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>SUSTAINABILITY MANAGEMENT</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>We believe sustainability is not only a commitment, but a key engine to drive growth and transformation. Our ESG strategy positions sustainability as a business challenge that drives our development and strengthens our management capacity. Based on the environmental, social and governance pillars (ESG) identified as key drivers, we move forward to a comprehensive and resilient management model. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>63</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>That is why our social investment programs strategy and development follow the guidelines of the United Nations 2030 Agenda. Through a thorough work we selected 9 of the 17 Sustainable Development Goals (SDG) considered priority for our company, to be able to focus and strengthen our efforts to contribute to specific goals.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#000000>The programs detailed below were promoted in line with SDG N&#176; 4 related to Quality Education:</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&#8220;<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Learn a Trade</B></FONT> <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Program in Bah&#237;a Blanca and Neuqu&#233;n</B></FONT>&#8221;. This program has been carried out since 2007 in cooperation with La Piedad Technical Institute (&#8220;ITLP&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish). Its aim is to impart training in trades to unemployed young people, empowering them through the acquisition of skills. It was expanded to Neuqu&#233;n with the cooperation of the San Jos&#233; Obrero School. It offers courses of carpentry, 3D printing, metal mechanic and basic welding. Since the launching of the program, we have trained over 1,500 persons.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Since its beginnings, this program has adopted new modalities and we encourage the training in trades at other organizations. One of these related lines, related to the SDG N&#176; 8 &#8220;Decent work and economic growth&#8221;, is <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>&#8220;Women in Circle&#8221;</B><B>.</B></FONT> This action seeks to generate an active commitment to gender equity in the community development. To such purpose, we sealed an Alliance with the Fundaci&#243;n Estudios Patag&#243;nicos and conducted two trainings in trades where the participation of women is sought.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Another initiative is <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>&#8220;More work, less recidivism&#8221;,</B></FONT> through which people deprived from their freedom can get access to training in trades, seeking to reduce their levels of recidivism through work opportunities. Thus, employment and decent work are promoted and training opportunities are offered through projects aiming at the responsible use of natural resources, boosting innovation and entrepreneur spirit, solidarity actions and social responsibility. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Schools Infrastructure Improvement Program </B></FONT>Started in 2018, this program aims at improving public educational institutions to increase graduation rates, reduce grade repetition and school drop-out and strengthen socio-emotional skills. We cooperate with government authorities and&#8212; after works are conducted&#8212; we impart awareness workshops to generate a sense of belonging among students. The program aims at strengthening educational quality and the local community. Over the last years, we have repaired over 20 educational facilities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Scholarship program &#8211;&nbsp;(&#8220;Haceme&#160;el Aguante&#8221;: support me):</B></FONT>&#160;This scholarship program, driven by <B>tgs</B>, is operated at the San Jos&#233; Obrero school in Neuqu&#233;n. Its objective is to increase opportunities for young people in vulnerability contexts. Scholarships comprise specialized areas such as Industrial Mechanics, Electricity, Carpentry and more. The equitable distribution of scholarships between genders shows our commitment to give a significant boost to education and the development of talented young people. &#160;We have increased scholarships by 20%, which in this year went up to 25. &#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>The Social Investment Programs we carry out reflect our commitment with the community where we are present, our objective to generate a positive impact on the people who live in the vicinities. In </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>64</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#242424'>light of that, we have broadened our look to integrate new approaches, such as development in culture, sports and personal and socio-economic aspects.&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#242424'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#000000>&#45;</FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:34.8pt></KBD><FONT style=color:#000000>The&#8221; </FONT><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Getting close to Basket&#8221;</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#000000> program, is focused on teenagers and the practice of sports as the means to socialize, gather and nurture good values. We offer basket classes to promote healthy habits and the overall development of teenagers. This action is carried out jointly with DUBA (Bah&#237;a United Disabled) an institution that works for the inclusion of disabled people in the practice of the different sports adapted to their abilities, helping them to better themselves, improve their self-esteem and integrate to society. </FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Committed to safety in the community, we executed several communication actions within the framework of <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Damage Prevention Programs</B></FONT>, with the aim of preventing incidents at natural gas facilities and pipelines. Actions included informative visits along with local firemen , trainings and talks with officers in charge of public works.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In June we presented the 13&#176; edition of the ESG Report, presenting GRI and SASB indicators, thus renewing once again our commitment to the Global Compact Principles. The 2023 ESG Report was acknowledged and prized at the Eikon&#160;Prizes and at the Social Ecumenic Forum.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>With reference to said ESG Report, this year we conducted a double materiality analysis, which led to the definition of the IROs that propose new material issues to be addressed in our oncoming ESG report. This year we also conducted a verification process to work on certain indicators.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>To conclude, we would like to highlight that through our Foundation, in 2024 we supported different non-profit organization projects, Fire Brigades, Hospitals, Indigenous&#160;Communities, etc. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-c) GOVERNANCE</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Corporate Governance</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>One of our main cornerstones is our ethical and transparent performance, </B></FONT>so we strive to implement the best Corporate Governance practices, in line with market international trends and local and foreign regulations in force, with the aim of protecting and maintaining our investors and the community on the whole and fostering transparency in our actions. Over the last years, the legal framework protecting minority investors has considerably widened, particularly in the United States and several European countries. Since then, Corporate Governance has become a matter of more and more relevance to ensure the transparency of the actions of any company listed in the Stock Exchange before the eyes of their investors and the community on the whole.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>65</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>TGS Governance and Auditing Structure </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Board of Directors</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Our Board of Directors runs the operations of our Company. <B>tgs</B> Bylaws stipulate that our Board of Directors shall be constituted by a minimum of nine regular directors and nine alternate directors and a maximum of eleven regular directors and eleven alternate directors. By virtue of the Corporate Bylaws and the Argentine Corporate Law, the Board of Directors shall meet at least once every three months.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>On December 18, 2019, <B>tgs&#8217; </B>Board of Directors<B> </B>approved its Internal Rules of Operations, which include guidelines for the diligent fulfillment of its duties.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Board of Directors currently in office was appointed by the Ordinary, Extraordinary and Special Shareholders Meetings dated April 17, 2024 (&#8220;Shareholders Meeting 2024&quot;) and its distribution of roles and responsibilities was also decided on the meeting held on the same date. It shall be in office until the Shareholders Meeting analyzes the financial statements as of December 31, 2024. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The members of <FONT style=font-size:10pt><B>tgs</B></FONT> Management as of the date of the issuance of the present Annual Report are:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_52.jpg width=692 height=115 alt='Picture 9' title='Picture 9'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Statutory Audit Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Statutory Audit Committee is our controlling body, set up in accordance with the provisions of Section 284 of Argentine Corporate Law. The Corporate Bylaws dictate the creation of an Audit Committee made up of three regular and three alternate members. In line with the Corporate Bylaws, two of the regular Statutory Auditors and their replacements must be appointed by Class &#8220;A&#8221; shares holders. The remaining Statutory Auditors and his/her replacements shall be appointed by the remaining holders of ordinary shares. The members of the Statutory Audit Committee are appointed at the Annual Ordinary Shareholders&#8217; Meeting and perform their roles for the period of one fiscal year, renewable for the same term.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_53.jpg width=539 height=88 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Executive Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Its role is to ensure an optimal corporate management under the terms of Section 269 of the Argentine Corporate Law. In accordance with the Corporate Bylaws&#8217; provisions, it is made up of by 4 members, who were appointed by the Board of Directors held on April 17, 2024 for the term of one year until the Shareholders Meeting considers the financial statements as of December 31, 2024. The current members are: Horacio Jorge Tom&#225;s Turri, Luis Alberto Fallo, Mar&#237;a Carolina Sigwald and Pablo Vi&#241;als Blake.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Audit Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>In accordance with the Argentine Public Offering Law, companies that offer their shares to the public must constitute an Audit Committee, a body of at least three regular directors and same number of alternate directors, most of which shall act independently. The present Audit Committee is as follows:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_54.jpg width=444 height=103 alt='Picture 12' title='Picture 12'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>

<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Corporate Governance Policies</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>We permanently seek to develop and improve our internal Policies and Procedures, adhering to the best international practices, with the main objective of protecting and enhancing our Company&#8217;s value for all our shareholders.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>67</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>tgs</B> Corporate Governance practices are regulated by applicable Argentine law (particularly, the Argentine Corporate Law N&#186; 19,550 and its amendments), the standards issued by the CNV and other entitled entities, our bylaws, as well as other documents approved by the <B>tgs</B> Board of Directors. Besides, as TGS is listed in the Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;) and its shares are listed in the New York Stock Exchange (&#8220;NYSE&#8221;), we are also subject to the standards and regulations of both the NYSE and the SEC.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Our Corporate Governance goals are to ensure:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Greater transparency in our performance, through the outlining of a culture of integrity and clarity in our business management.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Adequate supervision through the continuous improvement of our internal control structure, leadership in compliance with regulations and adoption of policies addressed to achieve effective risk management.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Proper allocations of accountability. Clear delineation of the scope of responsibility undertaken by the Board members and Managers of our Company, related to compliance with internal policies and regulations.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Among the measures implemented by <B>tgs</B> to ensure transparency in our actions, we can point out at &#8212;&nbsp;among others&#8212; the following components articulated into an Integrity Program, in accordance with the provisions of Law on Corporate Criminal Liability N&#176; 27,401:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Code of Conduct</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Our Code of Conduct governs the behavior of our Directors, Statutory Auditors, members of the Management and employees of both TGS and its controlled company. Furthermore, we seek to ensure acceptance of the Code by our suppliers, independent contractors, consultants and clients. The Code of Conduct outlines the indispensable ethics, transparency and honesty of people who work at the Company. It outlines several obligations and among other things, it declares the prohibition to forge or tamper with any information or documentation, behave in a manner that may generate damage to our Company or obtain any personal benefit that goes against the interests of the Company. It also stipulates that those who release information to the market must have full knowledge of and abide by CNV and SEC regulations and are under the obligation to report without misrepresentations.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=font-size:10pt;color:#00B0F0><B>Antifraud Policy</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> has issued an Antifraud Policy, the aim of which is to outline roles and responsibilities and detect irregularities that may arise in connection with the activities performed by our Company. Said Policy establishes the mechanisms devised for the reception of infringements reports, such as the Ethics Line through which employees, clients, suppliers and other stakeholders may anonymously inform about any violation to the Code of Conduct.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>68</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Dividends Distribution Policy</B></P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Through the Dividends Distribution Policy, the Board of Directors has set out guidelines that aim at maintaining an adequate balance between the amounts to be distributed as dividends and <B>tgs&#8217;</B> investment plans. Decisions shall be made seeking to ensure a reasonable management of business and balance in <B>tgs</B> financial condition.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Sustainability Policy</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>It provides the overall guidelines for conveying our sustainability vision into the development of our strategies and action programs, consistent with business continuity and promoting bonds with our stakeholders.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Best Stock Market Practices Policy </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>It has been adopted to ensure greater transparency in purchase or sale transactions or any kind of operations on negotiable securities listed in stock markets, preventing any person in our Company from obtaining any kind of advantage or economic benefit from the use of privileged information.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Policy of Related Parties&#8217; Approval and Disclosure </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>By means of this Policy, we have devised mechanisms to detect and disclose transactions with related parties, under the terms of Argentine Public Offering Law N&#176; 26,831. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Anti-Money Laundering Policy </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>This policy aims at preventing and detecting unusual or suspicious transactions, in accordance with the definitions of applicable law, accounting standards and international standards on this issue. It is applicable to all <B>tgs&#8217;</B> directors, statutory auditors and employees.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Policy of Recovery of Compensations Erroneously Awarded to Executive Officers in Financial Information</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>In 2023, we outlined this policy to meet the standards approved by the USA SEC for companies listed in NYSE and the National Association of Securities Dealers Automated Quotation (&#8220;NASDAQ&#8221;). The mentioned standards aimed at regulating an eventual recovery of compensations (bonds) erroneously awarded (&#8220;claw back&#8221;) to members of the Management Committee. Our policy was approved by the Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> on November 6, 2023</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style='font:10pt Verdana;color:#00B0F0'><B>Cybersecurity Policy</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>After the SEC issued new requirements that seek to regulate the information that companies disclose regarding material cybersecurity incidents as from December 18, 2023, at the Board of Directors Meeting held on November 6, 2023 we approved amendments to the Cybersecurity Policy, the Cybersecurity Incidents Management and the Systems Emergency Plan.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>69</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The policies seek not only to comply with the current regulations on the topic but also to provide transparency in the disclosure and manage cybersecurity incidents more efficiently. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Board of Directors expressly delegates in the Executive Committee the assessment of the incident materiality and if applicable the reporting of the incident. In order to determine cybersecurity incidents materiality, the following impacts shall be assessed: economic-financial or business, legal or regulatory, company reputation, people safety, quality and rendering of services, environment and /or stakeholders. We also conducted the annual revision of the cybersecurity risks matrix.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>tgs Risk Management</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>One of the pillars of <B>tgs</B> strategic planning is the permanent assessment of the environment, to guarantee the adaptation of the Company to an uncertain context in continuous transformation. Risk management is, in particular, one of the essential tools to fulfil the need to adapt to the environment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><B>tgs</B> has a Global Risk Management Policy approved by the Board of Directors of the Company. Said Policy establishes a continuous and systematic risk management that ensures treatment of them with effectiveness and efficiency, so as to guarantee an adequate follow-up of all those events that may negatively affect the objectives of the Company. Thus, this Policy develops a framework and a procedure for risk management in the different processes and activities of the Company, outlining criteria for identification, analysis, risk assessment as well as treatment and follow-up of same. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Policy establishes that the ultimate responsibility falls upon the members of the Management, whereas the responsibility for the implementation, maintenance and improvements corresponds to the Planning and Risk Department.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Company Management, based on its sustained commitment with risk management, seeks to include it in the organizational culture, integrating it to the usual management of each process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Audit Committee performs a regular monitoring of the company risk management, following up the results of the different performed assessments, the issuance of internal regulations, the advance status of undertaken plans and the several new developments on the matter.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>Among the risks managed by the Company, we can identify facilities&#8217; risks, projects&#8217; risks, environmental hazards and aspects, fraud risk, risk of corruption with the public sector, cyber security risks and strategic risks. The latter include all those risks whose occurrence may prevent the fulfillment of the Company strategic objectives and are monitored in a direct and permanent manner by the Management, to guarantee an adequate response through timely decision-making. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>Thus, the usual process of strategic risk management contemplates that once certain risk has been identified, the existent controls and mitigating factors must be surveyed, weighed and mapped based on impact and likelihood factors. We also determine the &#8220;risk appetite&#8221; or acceptable level so that &#8212;according to the gap between such risk level and the performed assessment&#8212;, we can establish the </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>70</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>need to implement specific action plans, which are then followed-up to determine their advance status and effectiveness in light of the pursued objectives.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><B>tgs</B> investments plan is based on risk prioritization, contemplating among others risks of different natures: operative, environmental, safety, impact on community, non-compliance with requirements, cybersecurity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>In 2024, we kept consolidating processes related to our corporate risk management, seeking to engage all the required areas in order to build a true risk management culture.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>On the topic of ESG (environment, social and governance), we conducted a double materiality analysis that led to the identification of several impacts, risks and opportunities related to sustainability. Within this framework, we created a new category labelled as &#8220;ESG Risks&#8221; in the Strategic Risk Matrix, which integrates risks previously identified and managed with new risks identified in the double materiality process. Thus, all the ESG risks will be followed up within the standard Strategic Risks process. On the other hand, in the year 2024 we assessed the physical risks derived from climate change, with the purpose of starting to delineate a climate resilience strategy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><B>7. Financial Assets</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Shareholding Structure as of December 31, 2024 </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> shares are listed in the Argentine Stock Exchanges and Markets (&#8220;BYMA&#8221;, according to its initials in Spanish) in the domestic market and in the form of American Depositary Receipts (&#8220;ADR&#8221;) &#8212;representing 5 shares each&#8212; in the NYSE, which are registered in the SEC. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2024, <B>tgs</B> celebrated 30 years of being listed in the NYSE, clearly a landmark that proves our transparent performance, financial soundness and sustained growth, which consolidates the Company as a key player in the regional energy industry. Being listed at the New York Exchange has been a main cornerstone to get&#160;access to international capital markets, which enabled us to expand our infrastructure, update our operations and be part of the growth of Vaca Muerta.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> has 794,495,283 issued shares, out of which 752,761,058 shares are in circulation. As of December 31, 2024, our treasury stock amounts to 41,734,225 of our own shares, representing 5.25% of our common stock. Our main shareholders are:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Class 3 Notes (&#8220;2031 Notes&#8221;)</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On July 24, 2024, <B>tgs</B> issued Class 3 Notes, denominated in United States dollars, for a face value of US$ 490,000,000, regulated under the Law of New York, accruing interest at a fixed nominal rate of 8.5% and due on July 24, 2031 (hereinafter, the &#8220;2031 Notes&#8221;). Such 2031 Notes were issued under the framework of a short-term and medium-term non-convertible bonds issuance program, with a maximum outstanding amount of up to US$ 2,000,000.000 (or its equivalent in other currencies), approved by the CNV. This notes placement was one of the first Argentine international transactions after many years with no Argentine issuances in the international capital market.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The terms of the 2031 Notes are:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-21.3pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_58.jpg width=439 height=216 alt='Imagen 42' title='Imagen 42'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The proceeds derived from this issuance were allocated to cancel the outstanding amount of the 2025 Class 2 Notes maturing in May 2025, conducted through a purchase bid launched simultaneously with the bond issuance and the anticipated redemption of the outstanding 2025 Bonds.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Financial leases</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On August 11, 2016 we entered an agreement with Petrobras (presently, Pampa Energ&#237;a) to finance the acquisition of assets belonging to the treatment and compression plant located in the area of R&#237;o Neuqu&#233;n. It consists in the payment of 119 consecutive monthly installments of US$ 623,457, not including taxes, and a purchase option for same amount payable upon the finalization of the 120<SUP>th</SUP> &nbsp;month of the term of the agreement.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>indebtedness&#8217;<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>maturities profile is as follows:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_59.jpg width=510 height=266 alt='Imagen 9' title='Imagen 9'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>74</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Financial Overview</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Throughout the year 2024, <B>tgs</B> maintained its sound financial condition. Our main sources of liquidity were the cash flows of our operations, our return to international capital markets with the issuance of the 2031 Bond mentioned above and access to first-rate entities credit lines, in view of the existing limitations and the macroeconomic conditions that marked the 2024. For further information, see, &#8220;<I>Financing Sources&#8221;.</I></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During the reported year, the BCRA continued limiting, though to a lesser extent than in 2023, the purchase of foreign currency for the payment of goods and services imports. However, <B>tgs</B> managed to obtain the currency required to conduct such payments, without significantly impacting business operations. For further information, see, &#8220;2. Macroeconomic Context - 2.1. &nbsp;Argentine scenario&#8221;. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Given the situation described in the preceding paragraphs, we had to obtain further financing for the amounts of US$ 54 million and EUR 1.2 million (after having cancelled US$ 88.1 million and all the balance of the debt denominated in EUR in the same year). As of December 31, 2024, our short-term indebtedness with renowned financial entities amounts to US$ 25.2 million.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In 2024, the United States dollar exchange rate went up by 27.7%, from $ 808.45 as of December 31, 2023 to $ 1,032 as of December 31, 2024. On the other hand, prices evolution measured by the CPI rose by 117.8% in year-over-year analysis.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Net cash flows applied to investment activities amounted to Ps. 384,898 million, mainly attributable to higher payments related to the acquisition of PPE given our commitment to the development of Vaca Muerta and financial collocations not considered as funds in accordance with the IFRS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In order to preserve the value of dollars in cash, funds are invested in low risk and high liquidity financial assets and in private bonds of first-class Argentine companies. In 2024 we prioritized dollar-linked funds collocations to mitigate the exchange rate risk in our net liabilities in dollars.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>To minimize the negative impact of the exchange rate on our financial indebtedness, <B>tgs</B> adopted a conservative approach. Hence, as of December 31, 2024 we have approximately 81% of funds collocations overseas denominated in United States dollars or adjusted by said variable.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>The table below presents our fund applications as of December 31, 2024:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_60.jpg width=383 height=155 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>75</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Throughout the years, our operation cash flows have been affected by the insufficient updating of natural gas transportation tariffs in spite of soaring inflation levels. The 675% tariff increase obtained as from April 2024 added to subsequent monthly increases will finally restore the financial situation of the Natural Gas Transportation Segment, provided the five-year tariff review is finalized.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It is worth highlighting the solid financial position we have been able to develop, based on a strong cash generation, a relatively low level of indebtedness and revenues mainly denominated in United States dollars in the Liquids Production and Commercialization and Midstream segments. However, the situations described in the present Annual Report, and the impact of the main national and international macroeconomic variables on international financial markets may negatively affect our financial condition and our clients&#8217; as well, which might derive into the deterioration of our capacity to generate cash to meet our financial obligations and capital expenditures. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In the short term, the most significant factors that impact on cash flows generation are: (i) international Liquid prices fluctuations, (ii) Liquids production levels, (iii) fluctuations in the price of natural gas used as PTR, (iv) natural gas production in Vaca Muerta, v) dollar exchange rate variations and vi) regulated Natural Gas Transportation segment tariffs.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>76</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-7.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8. ANALYSIS OF FINANCIAL CONDITION AND RESULTS OF OPERATIONS FOR THE YEAR ENDED DECEMBER 31, 2024</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following discussion of the financial condition and results of operations of the Company should be read in conjunction with the Company's consolidated financial statements as of December 31, 2024 and 2023, and for the years ended December 31, 2024 and 2023. These consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards (&#8220;IFRS&#8221;), issued by the International Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;IASB&#8221;) adopted by the Comisi&#243;n Nacional de Valores (&quot;CNV&quot;) through the provisions of Title IV, Chapter I, Section I, Article 1 &#8211;&nbsp;B.1 of the Rules of the CNV (&quot;New Text 2013&quot; or &quot;NT 2013&quot;) and considering Resolutions No. 1,660 and No. 1,903 issued by the ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>These consolidated financial statements were jointly audited by Price Waterhouse &amp; Co. S.R.L., and Pistrelli, Henry Martin y Asociados S.A.</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Effects of inflation</B></P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On December 3, 2018, Law No. 27,468 was enacted, sanctioned on November 15, 2018 by the National Argentine Congress. Among other measures, this law abolishes Presidential Decree No. 1,269/02 &#8212;amended by Presidential Decree No. 664/2003 &#8212;&nbsp;through which the controlling entities (among them the CNV) had been instructed not to accept inflation adjusted financial statements. On December 26, 2018, the CNV issued Resolution No. 777/2018, by virtue of which it instructs companies that offer their shares to the public to apply the financial statements restatement method to a stable currency in line with the provisions of IAS 29 &#8220;Financial Reporting in Hyperinflationary Economies&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In accordance with such standards, the restatement of financial statements was restarted as from July 1, 2018. In line with the restatement method, non-currency assets and liabilities are restated by an overall price index issued by the Argentine Federation of Professional Councils in Economic Sciences (&#8220;Argentine Federation&#8221;) since their acquisition date or last inflation adjustment (March 1, 2003).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, comparative information included in the financial statements has also been restated, but this fact has not modified the decisions based on the financial information corresponding to those fiscal years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For further information, see &#8220;Note 4. Significant Accounting Policies &#8211;&nbsp;d) Restatement to constant currency &#8211;&nbsp;Comparative Information&#8221; to the consolidated financial statements as of December 31, 2024.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>77</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8.a. Results of operations</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table presents a summary of the consolidated results of operations for the years ended December 31, 2024 (&#8220;FY2024&#8221;) and December 31, 2023 (&#8220;FY2023&#8221;):</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:-21.3pt;color:#000000'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_61.jpg width=619 height=217 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Activities of the Company in FY2024 and FY2023</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Revenues</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total revenues for FY2024 increased by Ps. 233,714 million compared to FY2023, mainly as a result of the increase in revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation and Midstream business segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_62.jpg width=544 height=170 alt='Imagen 17' title='Imagen 17'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Natural Gas Transportation</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During FY2024 revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation business segment accounted for 36% of <B>tgs</B> total revenues (22% for FY2023). Of the total revenues in this business segment, 84% and 82% corresponded to firm contracted capacity services for the years 2024 and 2023, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation segment during FY2024 reached Ps. 441,126 million (Ps. 215,700 million in FY2023). The increase of Ps. 225,426 million is due to the positive impact of transitional tariff increases received during 2024, as explained below. This effect was partially offset by the negative impact of inflation adjustment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On March 26, 2024, <B>tgs</B> and ENARGAS entered into a Tariff Transitory Adjustment Agreement (the &#8220;Tariff Agreement&#8221;), which provides for a 675% transitional adjustment in natural gas transportation </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>78</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>tariffs effective as of April 3, 2024, pursuant to Resolution No. 112/2024 issued by ENARGAS (&#8220;Resolution 112&#8221;). Resolution 112 provides that, as from May 2024 and until the RQT process is completed, the tariffs will be adjusted monthly based on a composite index (the &#8220;Transitional Adjustment Index&#8221;) by the wage index, the wholesale price index and the construction cost index.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During the following months, ENARGAS notified the licensee companies of the postponement of previously scheduled monthly adjustment. Additionally, it announced that the Transitional Adjustment Index would be replaced by an adjustment index based on expected inflation to be communicated by the Ministry of Economy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On August 1, September 2, October 1, November 4, December 4, 2024 and January 1 and February 1, 2025, ENARGAS issued new transition tariff charts contemplating increases of 4%, 1%, 2.7%, 3.5%, 3%, 2.5% and 1.5%, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the Company's tariff situation, see &quot;4. Businesses in 2024. Natural Gas Transportation&quot;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Liquids Production and Commercialization </B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment amounted to Ps. 556,662 million in 2024 (Ps. 21,311 million lower than in 2023). This negative effect was mainly due to the fall in prices and volumes of ethane sold and the lower volume of natural gasoline dispatched. These effects were partially offset by the higher volumes of propane and butane sold and the increase in international reference prices.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total volumes dispatched decrease by 51,836 tons or (5%). The table below shows the breakdown of the variation by product type and market:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_63.jpg width=549 height=124 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the evolution of the businesses in this business segment during 2024, see &#8220;4. Businesses in 2024. Liquids Production and Commercialization&#8221;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Midstream and Telecommunications</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Midstream and telecommunications revenues increased by Ps. 29,598 million in 2024 compared to 2023. This increase is due to higher revenues corresponding to natural gas transportation and conditioning services in Vaca Muerta. These effects were partially offset by the negative variation in the real foreign exchange rate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In October, the first module was put into operation, allowing for an increase of 6.6 MMm&#179;/d in the conditioning capacity of the Tratay&#233;n plant, reaching a total capacity of 21.4 MMm&#179;/d. It is worth noting that the entire project, which will require an investment of approximately US$ 350 million, will </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>79</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>enable <B>tgs</B> to have a total conditioning capacity of 28 MMm&#179;/d by the first quarter of 2025, when the final module, currently in the installation process, becomes operational.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the evolution of the businesses in this business segment during 2024, see &#8220;4. Businesses in 2024. Midstream and Telecommunications&#8221;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Cost of sales and administrative and selling expenses</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Cost of sales, administrative and selling expenses corresponding to FY2024 decreased by Ps. 30,936 million, 4.2% compared to the same period of the previous year. This variation is mainly due to the decrease in the cost of natural gas processed at the Cerri Complex (mainly due to a decrease in the price, measured in constant Argentine pesos). These effects were partially offset by higher: (i) fees and third-party services, (ii) repair and maintenance expenses, and (iii) taxes, rates, and contributions (mainly due to an increase in gross income tax and export duties resulting from higher revenues).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table shows the main components of operating costs, administrative and selling expenses and their main variations for FY2024 and FY2023:</P>

<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Reversal of impairment of property, plant, and equipment</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The reversal of impairment of property, plant, and equipment corresponds to the assets in the Natural Gas Transportation segment, in accordance with the projections and recoverability analyses conducted under International Accounting Standard 36 (IAS 36).</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Other operating results</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Other operating results experienced a positive variation of Ps. 2,468 million, mainly due to the recovery of provisions carried out in 2024 amounting to Ps. 3,843 million and the collection of insurance claims totaling Ps. 1,105 million. These effects were partially offset by the write-down of property, plant, and equipment assets amounting to Ps. 1,850 million.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>80</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Financial results</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In FY2024, financial results showed a positive variation of Ps. 180,050 million with respect to FY2023. The breakdown of net financial results is as follows:<A name=OLE_LINK10 /></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_65.jpg width=549 height=219 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This positive variation is mainly due to the lower negative net foreign exchange difference after the variation in the exchange rate registered in mid-December 2023. The selling peso/US dollar ended exchange rate closed at a value of Ps. 1,032.00 per US dollar as of December 31, 2024, representing an increase of approximately 28% (or Ps. 223.55 per US dollar) compared to the exchange rate observed at the closing of FY2023. As of December 31, 2023, said rate had increased by 356% (or $631.29 for each US dollar) regarding its quote at the closing of the FY2022. Meanwhile, <B>tgs</B>'s net liability position in US dollars was reversed in 2024, becoming an asset by the end of that year. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, as a result of the deceleration of inflation and the net active monetary position, the net monetary position recorded a loss in FY2024 that was Ps. 73,674 million lower than the loss recorded in Fiscal Year 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The effects mentioned above were partially offset by the negative variation in results generated by financial assets.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the cash flow used in investing activities, it amounted to Ps. 31,348 million compared to Ps. 53,685 million for FY2023. This effect was mainly due to the net repayment of financial debts in Fiscal Year 2024, whereas in Fiscal Year 2023, a net borrowing was recorded.</P>

<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-14.2pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=696 height=38 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During 4Q2024, the Company obtained comprehensive income of Ps. 129,619 million, compared to comprehensive loss of Ps. 51,090 million obtained in the same period of 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total revenues for 4Q2024 increased by Ps. 125,308 million compared to the same period of the previous year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation segment in 4Q2024 increased by Ps. 89,710 million, compared to 4Q2023. This positive variation was due to the tariff increases effective from April 1, 2024, of 675% nominal and the successive monthly tariff updates mandated by ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment, revenues increased by Ps. 38,434 million in 4Q2024, mainly as a result of higher volumes of propane and butane dispatched and the increase in international reference prices and butane sold in the local market. These effects were partially offset by the decrease in the exchange rate measured in homogeneous currency and the drop in the price of ethane.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the total volumes dispatched from the Cerri Complex, they increased by 25% or 67,732 tons, highlighting the increase in exported propane and butane.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The breakdown of volumes dispatched by market and product and revenues by market is included below:</P>
