<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21pt'><B>16.</B></KBD><B>FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1 Financial risk factors</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company&#8217;s activities and the market in which it operates expose it to a series of financial risks: market risk (including foreign exchange risk, interest rate risk, and commodity price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's risk management framework establishes that a risk map is determined that measures the potential impact of each of them on the financial situation and results of operations. Based on this, the Executive Officers are responsible for defining the policies, procedures, limits and measures aimed at mitigating the impact of said risks.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The sensitivity analyzes included below are based on the change in one of the factors while all others remain constant. In practice, this is unlikely to happen, and changes in several factors can be correlated, for example, in variations in the interest rate and variations in the foreign currency exchange rate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Sensitivity analysis only provides limited vision, at one point in time. The actual impact on the Company's financial instruments could vary significantly with respect to the impact shown in the sensitivity analysis.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.1 Risk associated with exchange rates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Exchange rate risk management</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In view of the main impacts of the aforementioned situation and those detailed in Note 1 to these Consolidated Financial Statements, the Company has implemented a series of measures to mitigate their impact. In this sense, the Company's Management permanently monitors the evolution of the situations that affect its business, in order to determine the possible actions to be taken and identify the eventual impacts on its equity and financial situation. The Company considers that its current financial position will allow it to comply, in the short term, with its foreign currency commitments. The Company's financial statements should be read in light of these circumstances.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company is primarily exposed to the fluctuation of the exchange rate of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine Peso due to the fact that almost its entire financial indebtedness is denominated in U.S. dollars. The exposure to other currencies is not significant.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As regards to the revenue derived from the Natural Gas Transportation segment, the tariffs charged by the Company are currently denominated in Argentine pesos. On the other hand, revenues in US dollars derived from the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment accounted for approximately 87% of the segment&#8217;s total revenues for the years ended December 31, 2024, 2023 and 2022. Total operating cost denominated in Argentine Pesos accounted for 74%, 81% and 83% for the years ended December 31, 2024, 2023 and 2022, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As of December 31, 2024, 2023 and 2022, 44%, 35% and 35% of total revenues are denominated in Argentine, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B>' financial risk management policies are defined with the objective of mitigating the impact of exchange rate fluctuations on the Company's foreign currency position. To this end, alternative investment evaluations are regularly carried out to diversify <B>tgs</B>' </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>51</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_128.jpg width=156 height=55 alt='Picture 6' title='Picture 6'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>investment portfolio among instruments denominated in U.S. dollars or, although denominated in Argentine pesos, to obtain positive returns in real terms.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Additionally, if deemed appropriate, the Company enters into derivative financial instruments that allow hedging the fluctuation of the U.S. dollar on the positions in such currency in the long term. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>However, the Company, in order to mitigate the impact on the future variation of the exchange rate, has placed funds in assets denominated in U.S. dollars. As of December 31, 2024, 81% of the Company's fund placements are denominated in U.S. dollars. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>For further information regarding the Company's foreign currency position see &quot;Note 18. Foreign currency assets and liabilities&quot;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Management of the Company estimates that, based on the net asset / liability position as of December 31, 2024 and 2023, a 10% appreciation in the exchange rate of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine peso, with all other economic-financial variables stable, could have resulted in a pre-tax income / (loss) of Ps. 10,758,875 and (Ps. 13,625,470), respectively. A 10% depreciation of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine peso would have an equal and opposite effect on the Statement of Comprehensive Income. This sensitivity analysis is theoretical as the actual impacts could differ significantly and vary over time.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In order to mitigate the exchange rate risk, during fiscal year 2024, <B>tgs</B> entered into both forward purchase operations of US dollars, as well as investments in mutual funds linked to the US dollar in order to hedge exposure to the risk associated with the exchange rate that derives from its financial debt.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.2 Interest rate risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Interest rate risk management seeks to reduce financial costs and limit the Company's exposure to increases in interest rates. <B>tgs</B>' exposure to risks associated with interest rate variations is limited given that all of its financial debt is subject to fixed interest rates. Information regarding the Company's financing is disclosed in Note 13.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In addition, the main objective of the Company's financial investment activities is to obtain the highest return by investing in low-risk and highly liquid instruments. The Company maintains a portfolio of cash equivalents and short-term investments comprised of investments in mutual funds and deposits in interest-bearing bank accounts, public and private securities. The risk of these instruments is low since they are mostly short-term and highly liquid in recognized financial institutions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As a consequence of the application of IAS 29, maintaining monetary assets generates loss of purchasing power, provided that such items are not subject to an adjustment mechanism that compensates to some extent the loss of purchasing power. This loss of purchasing power is included in the result of the period under gain on the net monetary position. On the contrary, maintaining monetary liabilities generates a gain in purchasing power, which are also included in such line item.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's risk management policies are defined with the objective of reducing the impact of the loss of purchasing power. During the 2024 and 2023 fiscal years the Company has maintained an asset net monetary position. As a consequence, <B>tgs</B> has recorded a net gain from exposure to inflation in the monetary items. In the fiscal year 2022, the Company maintained a liability net monetary position, resulting a net gain from exposure to inflation in the monetary items.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The following table shows a breakdown of the Company&#8217;s fixed-rate and floating-rate financial assets and liabilities as of December 31, 2024 and 2023:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In view of the nature of the Company&#8217;s financial assets which bear variable interest, an immediate 100 basis points decrease in the interest rate would not have a significant impact on the total value of the financial assets.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.3 Commodity price risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Commercial operations performed by the Company in its Liquids Production and Commercialization business segment are affected by a number of factors beyond its control, including changes in the international prices of the products sold, and government regulations on prices, taxes and other charges, among others.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The sales prices of exported propane, butane and natural gasoline are determined according to international reference prices (Mont Belvieu for propane and butane and NWE ARA for natural gasoline). Additionally, most of the total sales of propane and butane that are made in the domestic market are made at prices set by the Secretary of Energy for the different market segments. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>These prices have historically fluctuated in response to macroeconomic conditions and changes in supply and demand, which could affect <B>tgs</B>' profitability. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Based on volume of sales for the years ended December 31, 2024, 2023 and 2022, <B>tgs</B> estimated that, other factors being constant, a decrease of US$ 50 per ton in the international price of LPG and natural gasoline, respectively, would have decrease the Company&#8217;s net comprehensive income in its Liquids Production and Commercialization segment in Ps. 19,611,268, Ps. 29,490,323 and Ps. 21,749,155, respectively. On the other hand, an increase of US$ 50 per ton in the international price would have had the opposite effect.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.4 Credit risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>The Company&#8217;s exposures to credit risk takes the form of a loss that would be recognized if counterparties failed to, or were unable to, meet their payment obligations. These risks may arise in certain agreements in relation to amounts owed for physical product sales, the use of derivative instruments, and the investment of surplus cash balances. This risk mainly results from economic and financial factors or from a possible default of counterparty.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>The Company is subject to credit risk arising from outstanding receivables, cash and cash equivalents and deposits with banks and financial institutions, and from the use of derivative financial instruments. The Company&#8217;s policy is to manage credit exposure to trading counterparties within defined trading limits. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>53</P>

<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.5 Liquidity risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>This risk involves the difficulties that <B>tgs</B> may have in meeting its commercial and financial obligations. To this end, the expected cash flow is regularly monitored.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B> has policies for borrowing funds whose main objective is to cover financing needs at the lowest cost according to market conditions. One of the Company's main objectives is to have financial solvency. Given the current conditions of the financial market, the Company believes that the availability of resources and the positive cash flow from operations are sufficient to meet its current obligations, despite having credit lines for borrowing funds.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Additionally, a methodology is used for the analysis and assignment of credit limits to the different financial entities in order to minimize the associated liquidity risk. In line with this, the Company invests its liquid funds in financial entities with an adequate credit rating.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>

The amounts presented in the tables represent contractual undiscounted cash flows and, therefore, do not correspond to the amounts presented in the statement of financial position. These estimates are made on the basis of information available at the end of each year and may not reflect actual amounts in the future. Therefore, the amounts shown are provided for illustrative purposes only:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.6 Capital management risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's objectives in managing capital are to safeguard the Company's ability to continue as a going concern, to achieve an optimal cost of capital structure and to support the investment process in order to provide returns to shareholders and benefits to other stakeholders.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B> seeks to maintain a level of cash generation from its operating activities that will enable it to meet all of its commitments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company monitors capital on the basis of the leverage ratio. This ratio is calculated as total financial debt (including &quot;current financial debt&quot; and &quot;non-current financial debt&quot; as shown in the Statement of Financial Position) divided by total capital. Total </P>

<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>16.2.2</B><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'> </FONT><B>Fair value measurement hierarchy and estimates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>According to IFRS Accounting Standard 13, the fair value hierarchy introduces three levels of inputs based on the lowest level of input significant to the overall fair value. These levels are:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:6pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 1</B></FONT>: includes financial assets and liabilities whose fair values are estimated using quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets and liabilities. The instruments included in this level primarily include balances in mutual funds and public or private bonds listed on the <I>Bolsas y Mercados Argentinos S.A.</I> (&#8220;BYMA&#8221;). Mutual funds mainly invest in highly liquid instruments with low price risk.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 2</B></FONT>: includes financial assets and liabilities whose fair value is estimated using different assumptions quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (for example, derived from prices). Within this level, the Company includes those derivate financial instruments for which it was not able to find an active market.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 3</B></FONT>: includes financial instruments for which the assumptions used in estimating fair value are not based on observable market information.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>During 2024 and 2023, there were no transfers between the different hierarchies used to determine the fair value of the Company's financial instruments or reclassifications between categories of financial instruments.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The tables below show different assets at their fair value classified by hierarchy as of December 31, 2024 and 2023:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>17. REGULATORY FRAMEWORK</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>a.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Regulatory framework of the Natural Gas Transportation Segment</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>General Aspects</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Regarding <B>tgs</B>&#8217; Natural Gas Transportation business, it is regulated by Law No. 24,076 (&#8220;the Natural Gas Act&#8221;), its regulatory Decree No. 1,738/92 and the Regulatory framework for the transportation and distribution of natural gas in Argentina. The Natural Gas Act created ENARGAS, which is entitled, among other things, to set the basis for the calculation, monitoring, approval of tariffs and the power to verify compliance with the Natural Gas Law and its regulations. On January 28, 2016, Resolution No. 7 of the Ministry of Energy and Mining (&#8220;MINEM&#8221;) of Argentina repealed the Resolution 2000/2005 of the former Ministry of Federal Planning, Public Investment and Services (&#8220;ex MPFIPyS&#8221;) which provided that all tariff increases should have the prior intervention of the Undersecretary of Coordination and Management Control.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><B>tgs&#180; </B>License has been granted for an original period of 35 years beginning on December 28, 1992. However, the Natural Gas Act provides that <B>tgs</B> may apply to ENARGAS for a renewal of its license for an additional period of ten years. ENARGAS should evaluate at that time the performance of <B>tgs</B> and raise a recommendation to the Executive Branch. At the end of the period of validity of the license, 35 or 45, as appropriate, the Natural Gas Act requires a call for a new tender for the granting of a new license, in which <B>tgs</B>, provided the Company fulfilled substantially with the obligations resulting from the license, would have the option of matching the best offer that the Government received in this bidding process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Although the License expires in December 2027, it can be extended by the Executive Branch for an additional period of 10 years, provided that certain technical conditions established in it are met. Furthermore, in accordance with the regulations of the <I>Ley Bases</I>, the mentioned 10-year period is extended to 20 years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On September 8, 2023, the Company submitted to ENARGAS the request to initiate the procedure contemplated in Article 6 of the Natural Gas Law No. 24,076 for the renewal of the License. On June 13, 2024, ENARGAS issued a technical and legal report indicating that </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>the Company has fully complied with its obligations regarding the License. The aforementioned report allows the ENARGAS controller, after the non-binding public hearing held on October 21, 2024, to issue its recommendation report to be submitted to the PEN, which could ultimately issue the decree granting the required extension of the License. Following this submission of the ENARGAS recommendation, the PEN may issue the decree granting the extension of the License within 120 business days.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>As of the date of issuance of these Financial Statements, the PEN decree is still pending issuance, which is estimated to be issued during the first half of 2025 after the various public bodies have been involved.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Tariff situation</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Prior the enactment of the Public Emergency Law, and according to the Regulatory Framework, transportation tariffs were to be calculated in US dollars and converted into Argentine pesos at the time the customer was billed using the exchange rate prevailing at the date of the billing. The basic natural gas transportation tariffs charged by <B>tgs</B> had been established at the time of the privatization of GdE and were to be adjusted, subject to prior authorization, in the following cases: (i) semi-annually to reflect changes in the US producer price index (&#8220;PPI&#8221;) and (ii) every five years according to efficiency and investment factors determined by ENARGAS. The &#8220;efficiency factor&#8221; was a reduction to the base tariff resulting from future efficiency programs while the &#8220;investment factor&#8221; increased the tariffs to compensate the licensees for future investments which were not repaid through tariffs. Also, subject to ENARGAS approval, tariffs were to be adjusted to reflect non-recurrent circumstances or tax changes, other than income tax.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Following the enactment of the Public Emergency Law, the adjustment clauses for tariffs based on the value of the US dollar and those based on foreign price indexes, as well as any other indexing mechanism, were eliminated. Additionally, the law established an exchange rate of one Argentine peso equal to one US dollar for tariffs and granted the Executive Branch power to renegotiate public service contracts with the licensee companies according to certain criteria established therein. The law, after successive extensions, expired on December 31, 2017.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Between July 2003 and March 2018, the Company received a series of temporary tariff increases under the framework of the Integral Tariff Review (&#8220;RTI&#8221; for its acronym in Spanish) process initiated after the Public Emergency Law was passed.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>After being approved by the various intervening government agencies and the National Congress, the 2017 Integral Agreement signed on March 30, 2017 was ratified on March 27, 2018, through Decree No. 250/2018 (the &#8220;Decree 250&#8221;) of the Executive Branch. This decree represents the conclusion of the RTI process and the completion of the 2017 Transitional Agreement, and thus, the final renegotiation of the license after seventeen years of negotiations.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>The 2017 Integral Agreement sets the guidelines for the provision of the natural gas transportation service until the end of the License. Among these guidelines:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>The RTI Process, which will culminate in the signing of the integral agreement, was approved. As a result of this RTI, a new tariff schedule was also approved. This new tariff schedule applicable to the Company determined a total tariff increase of 214.2% and 37%, in the event that it had been granted in a single installment as of April 1, 2017, on the tariff of the natural gas transportation service and the CAU, respectively.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>A Five-Year Investment Plan is approved. Resolution 4362 obliged <B>tgs</B> for the execution of the Five-Year Plan, which requires a high level of essential investments for the operation and maintenance of the pipeline system, to provide quality, safe and reliable service. The Five-Year Plan shall be for the period from April 1, 2017 to &nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>61</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:71.45pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>March 31, 2022 and will amount to Ps. 6,786,543, expressed in December 31, 2016 currency terms. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>A non-automatic six-month adjustment mechanism for the natural gas transportation tariff and the investment commitments were approved. This adjustment must be approved by ENARGAS and for its calculation, the evolution of the WPI published by INDEC will be considered. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD><B>tgs</B> and its shareholders must withdraw any claim against the Government related to the natural gas transportation business, including the arbitration proceedings before the ICSID. The Company desisted from it on June 26, 2018.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Semi-annual tariff increase</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>As mentioned above, following the completion of the RTI process, the Company is granted the right to receive a semi-annual tariff adjustment, which is subject to the authorization of ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>In the public hearing held on September 4, 2018, in which the Company requested, based on the variation of the WPI recorded for the period February - August 2018, a tariff increase of approximately 30%. Considering the hearing, on September 27, 2018, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 265/2018 which determined a 19.7% tariff increase effective as of October 1, 2018.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>This increase was determined by ENARGAS based on the simple average of the WPI, the Construction Cost Index for the period February and August 2018 and the Salary Variation Index between December 2017 and June 2018.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On March 29, 2019, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 192/2019 (&quot;Resolution 192&quot;) that approved, effective as of April 1, 2019, a 26% increase in the tariff chart applicable to the public service of natural gas transportation by <B>tgs</B> in force as of March 31, 2019.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In accordance with current regulations, ENARGAS has considered the evolution of the wholesale price index update index (&quot;WPI&quot;) between the months of August 2018 and February 2019, in order to define the semi-annual adjustments applicable to <B>tgs</B> tariffs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Regarding the semi-annual tariff adjustment that was to be enforced as from October 1, 2019, on September 3, 2019, the SE issued Resolution No. 521/2019 (&#8220;Resolution 521&#8221;) postponing it to February 1, 2020,<FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> </FONT>subsequently modified by Resolution No. 751/2019 by which it was postponed until February 1, 2020. This deferral also meant that the Company has adapted, in equal proportion to the income that is not received, the execution of the Five-Year Investment Plan.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Following the enactment of the Solidarity Law, the national government announced its intention to suspend tariff adjustments for public services for the transportation and distribution of natural gas and electricity under federal jurisdiction for 180 days, in order to initiate a process of renegotiation of the RTI, or to initiate an extraordinary tariff review. On June 18, 2020, the PEN promulgated Decree of Necessity and Urgency No. 543/20, through which the tariff freeze established by the Solidarity Law was extended for another 180 calendar days.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 16, 2020, Decree of Necessity and Urgency No. 1,020/2020 (&#8220;Decree 1,020&#8221;) was issued, through which the start of the renegotiation of the RTI concluded in 2018 within the framework of the Solidarity Law was determined.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>According to that decree, the renegotiation period could not exceed 2 years from the date of entry into force. Until then, the current renegotiation agreements were suspended. Said renegotiation remained in the power of ENARGAS ad-referendum of the PEN. The </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>renegotiation process was extended on successive occasions, the last of which was until July 9, 2025.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Additionally, the Solidarity Law provides for the administrative intervention of ENARGAS, recently extended through Decree 1,023/2024 (&#8220;Decree 1,023&#8221;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On March 16, 2021, the public hearing convened by ENARGAS was held for the purposes of considering the Transitional Tariff Regime (&#8220;RTT&#8221;) in accordance with the provisions of Decree 1,020. In this regard, <B>tgs</B>, without renouncing the full percentage of tariff recomposition that corresponds to it, alternatively presented at this hearing a proposal for a tariff increase calculated by 58.6%, as of April 1, 2021. It was calculated based on the financial needs to meet the operating and financial costs, capital investments and taxes, which were calculated considering the evolution of the inflation rate for a period of 12 months from its inception. Such requested increase contemplated only the necessary funds to meet the obligations as a licensee.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Additionally, in such public hearing, <B>tgs</B> denied and dismissed the arguments raised in it that considered that the current natural gas transportation tariff is not fair and reasonable given the alleged existence of serious defects in the administrative acts derived from the processing of the last Regime Comprehensive Tariff (&#8220;RTI&#8221;) made for <B>tgs</B>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On April 28, 2021, ENARGAS sent to <B>tgs</B>, a Transitory Agreement Project 2021 within the framework of the provisions of Decree 1,020. Said project provided:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; The non-granting of a temporary tariff increase, keeping the tariff schedules approved by ENARGAS in April 2019 unchanged.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; It established that from May 2021 and until the Definitive Renegotiation Agreement is in force, ENARGAS will proceed to re-calculate the transportation tariffs at that time in force with effect from April 1, 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; It did not establish a mandatory investment plan.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt;color:#000000'>&#8226; The prohibition of: (i) distribution of dividends, (ii) an early cancellation of financial and commercial debts contracted with shareholders, and (iii) the acquisition of other companies or granting loans.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On April 30, 2021, through a note sent to that body, <B>tgs</B> stated that, given the context in which it develops its activities and the proposed terms and conditions, it was not feasible for the Company to sign the 2021 Temporary Agreement Project.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On June 2, 2021, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 149/2021 (the &#8220;Resolution 149&#8221;) approving an RTT for the year 2021 (the &#8220;RTT 2021&#8221;) for <B>tgs</B> effective as of that date. On the other hand, the Ministry of Economy and ENARGAS issued Joint Resolution No. 1/2021 where they approved the actions taken in the renegotiation process developed by ENARGAS in the terms of Decree No. 1020/2020, indicating that it was not feasible to arrive to an agreement on a transitional tariff adjustment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In July 2021, <B>tgs</B> filed appeals for reconsideration with the PEN, the Ministry of Economy of the Nation and ENARGAS with an appeal or hierarchical subsidy in accordance with the respective powers of each of these bodies in the dictation of the entire regulatory plexus associated with Resolution 149, (Decree 1020/20, Decree 353/21, and Joint Resolution No. 1/2021), requesting that the RTT be declared null and void and the RTI be restored. We recall that the RTT began with Decree 1020/20, which set the guidelines to carry out the renegotiation of the RTI, later issuing Joint Resolution 1/21, which sets the RTT, and Decree 353/21, which approved Joint Resolution 1 /21, and culminates with Resolution 149, which approved the new tariff charts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The challenges are based on: i) the illegality of Decree 1020 because it does not follow the lines of the delegation provided for in Law 27,541, and as a Decree of Necessity and Urgency it does not comply with the requirements of the Constitution for the issuance of this regulation; ii) the extension of the emergency beyond that provided by Congress; iii) the </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>failure to carry out the tariff renegotiation as provided for in Law 24. 076; iv) the disregard of the principle of fair and reasonable tariffs, and the acquired rights of <B>tgs</B> in the License, the Contractual Adjustment Agreement and the RTI; and v) the suspension of the RTI for reasons of public interest, which merits the recognition of the compensations, provided by both the Administrative Procedures Law and the Basic Rules of the License.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The restrictions imposed on the management and management of the Company, which have no legal justification, are also questioned, given that the emergency declared by Law No. 27,541 only empowered the National Executive Power to renegotiate the RTI and not the License.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The challenges and the request for reinstatement of the RTI have been made without prejudice to the right of <B>tgs</B> to pay the compensation that corresponds to it for not having complied with the RTI since April 2019.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On November 15, 2021, the Company filed a Prior Administrative Claim before ENARGAS and the Ministry of Economy. The purpose of said presentation is to request the compensation that corresponds to <B>tgs</B> for the lack of application of the semi-annual adjustment methodology established in the RTI approved by Resolution 4362 between October 1, 2019 and June 1, 2021.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In addition, payment of compensation for the damages suffered by <B>tgs</B> due to the freezing caused by the failure to apply the six-monthly adjustment methodology established in the RTI in the aforementioned period is requested.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On January 19, 2022, a new public hearing was held, within the framework of ENARGAS Resolution No. 518/2021, whose purpose was to deal with a transitory tariff adjustment within the framework of Decree 1020/2020. In such hearing, with the purpose of reaching a definitive agreement on the renegotiation and recomposing the license economic-financial equation, <B>tgs</B> requested a transitory tariff adjustment applied in two stages for the year 2022 for a total of 106%, due to the evolution of operating costs and the main macroeconomic indicators.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Subsequently, on February 1, 2022, <B>tgs</B> received from ENARGAS a proposal for a Transitional Renegotiation Agreement (the &#8220;2022 Transition Agreement&#8221;) which was approved by our Board of Directors on February 2, 2022 and by the corresponding government agencies on February 18, 2022. The 2022 Transition Agreement includes, in some aspects, similar terms to the 2021 RTT with the particularity that it grants <B>tgs</B> a 60% tariff increase effective as from March 1, 2022 (the &#8220;RTT 2022&#8221;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The 2022 Transition Agreement was ratified by the PEN through Decree No. 91/2022, which entered into force as from February 23, 2022. On February 25, 2022, the ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 60/2022 by which it put into effect the tariff schedules that contemplate the RTT 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>It should be noted that, in accordance with the provisions of the 2022 Transition Agreement, <B>tgs</B> committed not to initiate new claims, appeals, actions, lawsuits or demands of any kind; and/or to suspend, keep suspended or extend the suspension of all appeals and claims filed that are related in any way to the Renegotiation of the Comprehensive Tariff Review in force, Law No. 27,541, Decrees No. 278/20 and No. 1020.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Under Decrees 1020 and 815, on December 7, 2022, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 523/2022 which called for a public hearing on January 4, 2023 to, among other issues, consider the transitory adjustment of tariffs for the public natural gas transportation service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On March 16, 2023, the TGS Board of Directors approved the proposed addendum to the transitional renegotiation agreement (the &quot;2023 Transition Agreement&quot;) sent by ENARGAS. This addendum was subsequently ratified by the PEN through Decree No. 250/2023 of April </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>64</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2024 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>29, 2023. Previously, on April 27, 2023, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 186/2023 through which the new tables were published. current tariffs.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The 2023 Transition Agreement has similar conditions to the 2022 Transition Agreement and includes:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- As of April 29, 2023, a temporary rate increase of 95% on the natural gas transportation rate and the CAU.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- During its term, <B>tgs</B> could only distribute dividends after requesting authorization from ENARGAS, subject to the approval of the Ministry of Economy.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 14, 2023, ENARGAS Resolution No. 704/2023 called for a public hearing to be held on January 8, 2024 in order to consider a temporary tariff adjustment. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 16, 2023, decree No. 55/2023 was issued declaring the emergency of the national energy sector until December 31, 2024 which was extended until July 9, 2025 by Decree 1023 of November 19, 2024. Among other issues, this decree: (i) establishes the beginning of the process of Five-year Tariff Review (&#8220;RQT&#8221;), (ii) the intervention of ENARGAS as of January 1, 2024 and (iii) instructs the Ministry of Energy to issue the necessary standards and procedures for the sanction of market prices for the public transport service of natural gas. Decree 1023 provides that the entry into force of the tariff tables resulting from the tariff review initiated pursuant to the provisions of Decree No. 55/23 may not exceed July 9, 2025.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>After the public hearing was held on January 8, 2024 and its validity was declared by ENARGAS, On March 26, 2024, <B>tgs</B> entered the 2024&#8217;s Transitional Agreement with the ENARGAS, which provides for a transitional 675% increase in natural gas transportation tariffs. This tariff increase came into effect on April 3, 2024, after Resolution No. 112/2024 (the &#8220;Resolution 112&#8221;) issued by ENARGAS was published in the Official Gazette. According to Resolution 112, as from May 2024 and until the RTI process is completed, tariffs are adjusted monthly according to the following formula (the &quot;Transitional Adjustment Index&quot;):</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- 47.0% adjusted by the Wage Index - Registered Private Sector published by INDEC</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- 27.2% adjusted by the Wholesale Price Index (&#8220;WPI&#8221;)</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- 25.8% adjusted by the Construction Cost Index in Greater Buenos Aires - Materials chapter</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>published by INDEC.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>To this end, ENARGAS will issue the corresponding monthly resolution adjusting the tariff tables to be applied. On May 9, 2024, ENARGAS notified the licensee companies of the public natural gas transportation and distribution service of the postponement of the monthly tariff adjustment mentioned above.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>During the months of May to July 2024, ENARGAS notified <B>tgs</B> that it will postpone the implementation of the monthly tariff adjustment. Likewise, it notified that it will replace the monthly adjustment methodology mentioned above for the remainder of 2024. According to the ENARGAS notification, the monthly tariff increase will be based on the expected inflation to be estimated by the Ministry of Economy for said period. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On July 1, ENARGAS once again informed the Company of the postponement of the monthly</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>tariff increase, this time corresponding to the month of July, maintaining the tariff schedules in effect since April 3, 2024. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>During the year 2024 and the elapsed period of 2025, the Company received tariff increases of 675%, 4%, 1%, 2.7%, 3.5%, 3%, 2.5%, and 1.5%, effective from April 3, August 1, September 2, October 1, November 4, December 4, January 1, 2025, and February 1, 2025, respectively.</P>

<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
